## Q46: Deep dive on Mushroom farming: why is it popular for XP, how much does it earn, and what is the best setup?

**Answer:**

Mushroom farming is a cornerstone of the 2026 Hypixel SkyBlock farming meta, particularly valued for its high experience yields and its synergy with the lucrative pest farming system.

### **Why it is Popular for XP**
Mushroom farming is widely considered the **best method for leveling the Farming skill** because breaking mushrooms provides the **most experience per crop broken** compared to any other plant in the Garden [1]. 

To maximize these XP gains, players often use the **Rabbit Pet**, which provides up to 30 Farming Wisdom at level 100 [2]. Additionally, the **Blessed reforge** on farming tools is favored for those prioritizing progression over raw coins, as it grants additional Farming Wisdom [3].

### **Earnings and Profitability**
Mushroom farming is highly profitable, largely because it serves as a primary method for **Pest Farming**, which is currently one of the top money-making methods in the game [4, 5].

*   **Hourly Rates:** A maxed setup can earn between **40M and 50M coins per hour** baseline [6, 7]. During **Mayor Finnegan's** term, these rates can spike to **65M–90M coins per hour** due to reduced pest cooldowns and specific perks [4, 7, 8].
*   **Core Profit Drivers:**
    *   **Pest Drops:** Snails, which spawn during farming, are currently noted as the most profitable pests [9].
    *   **Burrowing Spores:** These are very rare drops specifically from mushrooms and are used to apply the "Rooted" reforge to equipment [10].
    *   **Mushroom Cow Synergy:** Using a **Mooshroom Cow pet** provides an additional mushroom drop every time you break a crop, further increasing the raw material yield [11].
    *   **Tool XP Capsules:** Maxed tools (level 50) generate XP tool capsules every 200k tool XP, which can be sold for extra passive hourly profit [12].

### **The Best Setup**
For optimal efficiency and profit, the 2026 "hypermaxed" setup consists of the following:

#### **1. Armor**
*   **Set:** **Helanthis Armor** is the current best-in-slot for endgame farming, requiring Farming level 50 to upgrade from Fermentto [1, 13].
*   **Boots:** **Rancher's Boots** are essential for precisely controlling movement speed (tuning to meta speeds like 232% for certain layouts) [14, 15].
*   **Reforge:** **Mossy** (applied via Overgrown Grass) for maximum Farming Fortune [16].

#### **2. Farming Tools**
*   **Primary Tool:** The **Fungi Cutter**, upgraded through three tiers using Jacob's Tickets and mushrooms [10, 17, 18].
*   **Enchantments:** **Harvesting VI**, **Dedication IV**, **Turbo-Mushrooms V**, and the ultimate enchantment **Crop Fever** [3, 19].
*   **Reforge:** **Bountiful** for raw coin gain or **Blessed** for XP [3, 18].

#### **3. Equipment & Accessories**
*   **Equipment Set:** The **Blossom set** provides the highest Farming Fortune, while the **Lotus set** is a viable mid-game alternative [20, 21].
*   **Reforge:** **Rooted** on all equipment pieces [10].
*   **Accessory Line:** The **Copper line** of accessories is recommended to increase the frequency of rare visitors [22, 23].

#### **4. Pets**
*   **Yield/Fortune:** The **Mooshroom Cow** (level 100 Legendary) is preferred for crop yield as its fortune scales with player Strength [11].
*   **Pest Spawning:** The **Mosquito Pet** (with a Brown Bandana) is used to maximize "Bonus Pest Chance" [21].
*   **Pest Killing:** The **Hedgehog Pet** is swapped in when vacuuming pests to gain a massive +100 Farming Fortune bonus specifically for pest loot [24].

#### **5. Technical Optimizations**
*   **Infinity Vacuum Hooverous:** The final vacuum upgrade is required to effectively clear pests and one-shot Earthworms when paired with a Hedgehog pet [25, 26].
*   **Squeaky Mouse Mat:** This item allows you to lock your "yaw and pitch" (angles) to maintain max efficiency (20 blocks per second) without manual micro-adjustments [27, 28].
*   **Garden Chips:** Prioritize maxing the **Vermin Vaporizer** chip for +100 bonus pest chance and the **Hypercharge** chip to buff temporary fortune boosts from NPCs like Phillip [29, 30].

---

## Q47: Deep dive on Pumpkin and Melon farming: are they worth doing for coins, what is the setup, and when do they become viable?

**Answer:**

Based on the sources, farming Pumpkin and Melon has transitioned from a basic leveling method to a cornerstone of both the **passive Greenhouse meta** and the **seasonal Harvest Feast event** in 2026.

### **Are they worth doing for coins?**
Yes, but their value depends on the method of farming:

*   **Passive Income (Greenhouse):** Unlocking and running a Greenhouse with **Gloom Gourds** (a mutation created by planting pumpkins and melons adjacently) was initially making over **100 million coins per day** [1]. While prices for materials like Ethereal Vines have dropped significantly (from 20M to 30k), the Greenhouse remains a viable passive method, capable of netting around **19 million coins per day** with minimal effort [1, 2].
*   **Active Profit (Pest Farming):** Farming any crop (including pumpkin/melon) to spawn pests is currently one of the strongest money-making methods, yielding **40–60 million coins per hour** normally, and up to **90 million coins per hour** during Mayor Finnegan [3, 4].
*   **Seasonal Events (Harvest Feast):** During the Harvest Feast (Autumn), farming these crops allows you to drop rare materials like **Overpriced Drink** (from Pumpkins) and **Melon Juice** (from Melons) [5, 6]. These are used for high-tier reforges and mixins that remain in high demand [5, 7].

### **The Setup**
To maximize efficiency, you should aim for the following progression:

**1. Tools and Equipment:**
*   **Designated Tools:** Use the **Pumpkin Dicer** and **Melon Dicer** (Tiers 1.0–3.0) [8]. For secondary stats, the new **Overpriced reforge** (from pumpkin drops) is meta for finding rare crops [5].
*   **Armor:** Start with **Melon Armor** (unlocked at Garden Level 6), which is the base for all high-tier farming sets [9, 10]. Progress through Crappie, Squash, and Fermentto armor until you reach **Helianthus Armor** (Farming Level 50 required) [10-12].
*   **Equipment:** Use the **Lotus set** early, upgrading to the **Blossom set** for maximum farming fortune [13, 14].

**2. Pets:**
*   **Early Game:** Use a **Rabbit pet** for the 30% farming wisdom boost to level up quickly [15].
*   **Late Game Fortune:** Use a **Legendary Elephant** with a Green Bandana or a **Mushroom Cow** (if you have high strength) for maximum fortune [16, 17].
*   **Pest Farming:** A **Hedgehog pet** is essential for killing pests efficiently, while a **Slug or Mosquito pet** should be equipped when trying to spawn them [14, 18, 19].

**3. Garden Utilities:**
*   **Sundial:** Highly recommended for full armor sets (like Helianthus) to lock your movement speed at the optimal "meta speed" for each crop [12, 16].
*   **Vacuum:** Use an **Infinitive Vacuum Hooverus** with the **BD reforge** to handle pests without losing farming efficiency [20, 21].

### **When do they become viable?**
Progression is gated by Garden and Farming levels:
*   **Early Viability (Garden Level 1–6):** You unlock the Garden at **SkyBlock Level 5** [22]. Melons specifically become farmable at **Garden Level 6**, which also allows you to craft **Melon Armor**, the first set to provide rare crop drop chances [9, 10].
*   **Passive Viability (Garden Level 12):** The **Greenhouse** unlocks at Level 12 [20]. This is the point where you can start generating "semi-AFK" profit through mutations like Gloom Gourds [23].
*   **Endgame Viability (Farming Level 40–60):** To use the most profitable gear (Fermentto/Helianthus), you must reach **Farming Level 40 or 50** [12]. At this stage, you can participate in the **Harvest Feast** event at maximum efficiency, where seasonings and rare crafting materials become major profit drivers [24, 25].

---

## Q48: Deep dive on Nether Wart farming: setup, coins/hr, and how it compares to other crops.

**Answer:**

Nether Wart farming in 2026 has evolved from a basic "click and farm" activity into a multi-layered system involving **Greenhouse mutations**, **pest hunting**, and **seasonal event mechanics** [1-3]. 

### **The Setup: Gear, Tools, and Pets**
To farm Nether Wart effectively, you must progress through specific Garden armor tiers and utilize specialized tools.

*   **Armor Progression:**
    *   **Squash Armor:** Essential for mid-game; farming Nether Wart while wearing this set grants a chance to drop **Fermentto**, a rare material required to craft the next tier [4].
    *   **Fermentto Armor:** The standard high-level set, which is eventually upgraded into **Helianthus Armor** at Farming Level 50 [4, 5].
    *   **Reforges/Enchants:** The **Mossy** reforge (from Overgrown Grass) is the best for armor [6]. **Pest VI** should be applied to all pieces if you are focusing on pest farming profit [7].
*   **Specialized Tool:** 
    *   The **Newton Nether Warts Hoe (T1–T3)** is the designated tool for this crop [8]. 
    *   **Hypermaxing:** Aim for level 50 (via tool XP capsules), **Crop Fever 5**, **Dedication 4**, and **Harvesting VI** [9, 10]. The **Blessed** reforge is recommended for secondary tools, while **Bountiful** is common for primary profit tools [10, 11].
*   **Equipment:** The **Blossom set** provides the highest farming fortune [12, 13]. **Rooted** is the best reforge for equipment [14].
*   **Pets:** 
    *   **Mushroom Cow (Legendary):** The best for raw farming fortune, provided you maximize your **Strength** stat [15]. 
    *   **Hedgehog (Level 100):** Crucial for killing pests spawned while farming, as it provides an additional **+100 Farming Fortune** on pests and allows you to one-shot earthworms [16].

### **Greenhouse Strategy: The Ashreath Mutation**
The Greenhouse update (Dec 2025) introduced **mutations**, which are now the superior method for raw Nether Wart generation [2].

*   **Ashreath:** This mutation is specific to Nether Wart. When harvested, it gives a base of **720 Nether Wart** before Farming Fortune or yield bonuses are applied [2].
*   **Efficiency:** Ashreath is approximately **20% better per harvest** for raw NPC coins compared to other immediately harvestable mutations like Gloom Gourd or Witherloom [17].
*   **Yield Optimization:** To maximize profit, you should unlock all three Greenhouse plots (costing roughly 100M coins in **Ethereal Vines** and compost) [18]. A maxed setup with a 1 hour 48 minute growth stage timer allows for continuous harvesting [19].

### **Coins per Hour and Profitability**
Profit varies significantly based on whether you are farming "lazy" or using hyper-optimized methods.

*   **Greenhouse Semi-AFK:** Logging on for 5 minutes every few hours to clear mutations can net roughly **30M coins comfortably overnight** [18]. 
*   **Active Pest Farming:** Active Garden farming (focused on pests) is one of the top money-making methods in Q1 2026. 
    *   **Outside Finnegan:** ~40M–60M coins/hr [1, 20].
    *   **During Mayor Finnegan:** ~65M–90M coins/hr with a maxed setup [1, 21].
*   **Tool Capsules:** A level 50 hoe generates an XP tool capsule every 200k tool XP, adding an extra **~2.5M coins/hr** in passive value [9].

### **Comparison to Other Crops**
Nether Wart holds a unique niche in the current meta compared to other agricultural options:

*   **XP vs. Money:** While Nether Wart is highly profitable via mutations, **Mushrooms** remain the best crop for raw **Farming XP** per crop broken [5].
*   **Bazaar vs. NPC:** Crops like **Sunflower** and **Moonflower** are often more profitable on the Bazaar due to high demand for new 2026 items [22]. Nether Wart remains a "safe" option for raw coins sold to NPCs via high Ashreath yields [17, 23].
*   **Harvest Feast (Seasonal):** During the **Harvest Feast** event, Nether Wart has a 1/18,000 chance to drop **Jacob’s Participation Medal** [24]. However, community feedback labels this as one of the "worst items" because it buffs farming minions, which are currently considered useless compared to active farming or the Greenhouse [25, 26].

---

## Q49: Deep dive on Cocoa Bean farming: is it worth it, what is the setup, and how does it compare?

**Answer:**

In the early 2026 meta, **Cocoa Bean farming** has transitioned from a niche activity into a foundational component of the high-profit **Pest Farming** and **Pest Hunting** strategies. While the crop itself provides standard agricultural returns, its primary value lies in its role as a vehicle for spawning pests and obtaining rare expansion materials [1, 2].

### **Is it Worth It?**
Yes, Cocoa Bean farming is currently considered "worth it" for two primary reasons:

*   **Pest Farming Profitability:** Pest farming (often done while farming Cocoa) is one of the most consistent and accessible money-making methods, capable of generating **60M to 90M coins per hour** during Mayor Finnegan’s term, and **40M to 50M** normally [1-3]. 
*   **Unique Returns:** Farming Cocoa with **Crappie, Squash, or Fermento armor** allows you to drop **Squashes**, a valuable rare material [4, 5]. Additionally, the new **Harvest Feast event** (occurring in Autumn) introduces **Feast Cocoa Drops**, which are required for high-tier items like the **Bug Blender** (which grants **+100 Farming Fortune on pests**) and the **Disco Destination** [6-8].

### **The Setup**
A modern Cocoa Bean setup requires a mix of specialized farming tools, speed management, and pest-optimization gear:

*   **The Tool:** The **Cocoa Chopper** is the designated tool, now categorized as a "Farming Tool" and capable of holding universal farming reforges like **Blessed** or **Bountiful** [9, 10]. Key enchantments include **Crop Fever**, **Cultivating**, **Dedication**, and **Harvesting** [11].
*   **Armor & Equipment:**
    *   **Armor:** Beginners should use **Fermento armor**, while endgame players use **Helanthus armor** (requires Farming 50) [12, 13].
    *   **Equipment:** The **Pest Hunter set** (including the **Pest Vest**) is essential for maximizing pest spawn chances [14, 15].
    *   **Speed Control:** You must use a **Sundial** (purchased for ~12M coins) given to Sam to lock your movement speed, as Cocoa requires specific speeds for maximum efficiency [11, 13].
*   **Pets:**
    *   **Mushroom Cow:** Used while farming the crop for maximum Farming Fortune [15].
    *   **Mosquito (with Brown Bandana):** Swapped to when aiming for maximum pest spawn chance [16, 17].
    *   **Hedgehog:** The primary pet for killing pests, providing a massive **+100 Farming Fortune on pests** [18, 19].
*   **Farm Design:** Modern setups utilize a **"glass ceiling"** or roof ten blocks above the farm. This forces pests to spawn in a predictable area, allowing you to quickly teleport up with an **Aspect of the Void** to catch them without disrupting your farming route [20, 21].

### **How Does It Compare?**
Cocoa Bean-based pest farming is one of the most competitive methods in the 2026 economy:

| Method | Estimated Yield (Coins/hr) | Effort/Gear Requirement |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Pest Farming (Cocoa/Wheat)** | **80M (Finnegan) / 40-50M (Base)** | High; requires maxed Garden and event active [2, 3]. |
| **Endgame Kuudra** | **70M - 90M** | Extremely high; requires Infernal gear [3]. |
| **Glacite Shafting** | **60M** | Very high; requires ~6B gear setup [3]. |
| **Greenhouse Mutations** | **40M - 50M** | Medium; semi-passive layout-based [3, 22]. |
| **Sorrow Grinding** | **7.5M - 15M** | Low/Medium; highly RNG dependent [3]. |

Compared to Mining (like Glacite Shafting), Cocoa/Pest farming is significantly **cheaper to set up** (hundreds of millions vs. billions of coins) while offering **comparable or superior hourly rates** that are less dependent on market fluctuations since pest drops can be sold directly to NPCs [1, 2]. However, community feedback notes that this grind is extremely time-intensive, with some calculations suggesting **57 hours of farming** are required to max out specific Feast event milestones [23, 24].

---

## Q50: What are the most important Farming collection milestones to reach first? Which collections unlock the best recipes or rewards?

**Answer:**

Based on the sources, the most important Farming collection milestones to reach first focus on establishing a foundation for mobility, storage, and your long-term armor progression.

### **Most Important Early Milestones**
For players starting their journey (Level 0–15), the priority is unlocking basic gear and utility items that improve quality of life:

*   **Wheat Collection III (Farm Suit):** This is recommended as your first armor set for early-game survivability and SkyBlock XP [1, 2]. 
*   **Wheat Collection (Farming Talisman):** A critical early goal to start building **Magical Power (MP)** [3]. Accessories like this provide permanent stat boosts through Maxwell in the Hub [4, 5].
*   **Wheat Collection (Agronomy Sacks):** Essential for managing inventory once it begins to fill with various crops and seeds [6].
*   **Carrot Collection 500 (Carrot Bait):** Necessary to fish up the **Carrot King**, which is required for specific progression and rewards like the Clover [7, 8].
*   **Melon Collection (Melon Armor):** Unlocked at Garden Level 6, this is one of the most vital milestones because Melon Armor is the base set that eventually builds into the best farming armor in the game [9, 10].

---

### **Best Collection Recipes and Rewards**
Collections unlock high-impact items that significantly boost your **Farming Fortune (FF)** or offer unique utility.

#### **Top Armor & Equipment Progression**
*   **The Armor Ladder:** Reaching high tiers in the **Melon, Pumpkin, and Mushroom** collections is required to climb the armor tiers: **Melon → Crappie → Squash → Fermento → Helianthus** [10, 11].
*   **Lotus Equipment:** Unlocked through Garden progression (bracelet at 50, belt at 100, necklace at 200, and cloak at 500 visitor offers), these pieces grant increasing fortune based on total visitor milestones [12].

#### **Harvest Feast & Specialized Utility**
The Harvest Feast event (running Early to Late Autumn) introduced powerful new recipes tied to specific crop materials:

*   **Cocoa Beans (Bug Blender):** An enchantment for your vacuum that grants up to **+100 Farming Fortune** when vacuuming pests, making it "goated" for pest farming [13, 14].
*   **Sunflower (Disco Destination):** A major quality-of-life item that reduces downtime for pest farming by filling your Garden with pests more efficiently [15, 16].
*   **Pumpkin (Turbo-Crop T7 & Overpriced Reforge):** Unlocks higher tiers of Turbo enchantments (+10 fortune per level) and the **Overpriced reforge**, which is expected to be the new meta for farming tools [17, 18].
*   **Potato (Hashbrown Reforge):** Unlocks the **Deep Fried reforge**, which grants 20 FF and increases the chance of finding Seasoning by 25% [13, 18].
*   **Carrot (Poignant Carrot Bait):** Used to upgrade the **Lucky Clover**, which provides extra Green Thumb fortune and Magic Find [8, 19].

#### **Greenhouse & Advanced Utility**
*   **Chloronite (Sundial):** Reaching milestones in Greenhouse mutations is necessary to craft the **Sundial**, which allows you to control your walking speed—a "broken" feature that lets you use full armor sets instead of switching to Rancher's Boots for speed control [20-22].
*   **Redstone Collection II (Accessory Bag):** While technically a Mining collection, it is a top priority for farmers to expand their Accessory Bag so they can benefit from Farming-related talismans without clogging their inventory [23, 24].
*   **Oak Collection IV (Small Storage):** A essential Foraging milestone for farmers, as it allows you to craft storage for minions to prevent them from stopping work when their small inventories fill up [25].

---

## Q51: Explain the Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) system completely. How does XP work, what are the 10 tiers, and what XP is needed for each?

**Answer:**

The **Heart of the Mountain (HOTM)** system is a specialized mining skill tree in Hypixel SkyBlock that allows players to unlock perks, abilities, and access to advanced mining zones [1].

### **How HOTM XP Works**
HOTM XP is the primary metric for advancing through the system's tiers. It is earned primarily through **Mining Commissions**, which are tasks issued by the King or the commission system in the Dwarven Mines [1, 2]. 

Key mechanics include:
*   **Daily Routine**: Completing commissions is the fastest way to progress [2]. 
*   **Daily Bonus**: The first four commissions you complete each day grant a significant **bonus of HOTM XP**, making consistent daily play the most efficient strategy for leveling [3, 4].
*   **Tokens of the Mountain**: Every time you reach a new HOTM tier, you earn **Tokens of the Mountain**, which are used to unlock new nodes (perks or abilities) on your skill tree [1].
*   **Mithril/Gemstone Powder**: While XP increases your tier, you must use Mithril or Gemstone Powder to upgrade the specific levels of the perks you have unlocked [1].
*   **Resets**: Your first HOTM reset is free, allowing you to reconfigure your tree without cost [5].

### **The 10 Tiers of Heart of the Mountain**
The system currently consists of **10 tiers** [6]. Progression through these tiers acts as a "ladder" that gates access to various items and areas [1, 4]:

*   **Tier 1**: The starting level upon entering the Dwarven Mines.
*   **Tier 2**: Unlocks the ability to forge the **Mithril Lantern** [7, 8].
*   **Tier 4**: A major milestone that unlocks access to the **Crystal Hollows** [3, 9].
*   **Tier 5**: Unlocks the ability to forge the **Titanium Lantern** [7, 8].
*   **Tier 7**: Often cited as a point where players focus on heavy "powder mining" to max out their perks [10].
*   **Tier 8**: Unlocks the ability to forge the **Glacite Lantern** [7, 8].
*   **Tier 10**: The current maximum tier [6]. It allows for high-level upgrades like the **Will-o-wisp** mining deployable, which requires a Glacite Lantern and a Caged Wisp [7, 8].

### **XP Requirements for Each Tier**
While the sources confirm there are 10 tiers and explain the methods for earning XP, the **exact numerical XP requirements for every individual tier** (e.g., the specific amount needed for Tiers 1 through 10) are not explicitly detailed in a single table within the provided documents. The sources primarily focus on Tier 4 as the major early-game goal for the Crystal Hollows [3, 9].

If you would like me to find the specific XP values for each of the 10 tiers, I can use a search tool to locate that exact data for you. Would you like me to do that?

## Q52: What HOTM nodes should players unlock first at HOTM 1-3? Which nodes give the most value early?

**Answer:**

For players progressing through **Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) levels 1 to 3**, the focus should be on nodes that increase resource yields and speed up collection grinding. The HOTM tree functions as a mining-specific skill tree where you spend **Tokens of the Mountain** to unlock perks and **Mithril Powder** to upgrade them [1].

The following nodes are recommended for early-game value:

### HOTM Tier 1
*   **Mining Speed:** This is the foundational node you must unlock first to progress down the tree [1]. It provides a passive increase to your mining speed, which is essential for breaking blocks in the Dwarven Mines efficiently [1, 2].

### HOTM Tier 2
*   **Mining Fortune I:** This is arguably the **most valuable node** at this stage [3]. It increases your chance to get multiple drops from a single block, which is critical for hitting collection milestones (such as the 60,000 Gold Ingot requirement for specific goals) [3, 4].
*   **Pickobulus (Active Ability):** This ability allows you to throw an explosive pickaxe that mines all blocks within a small radius [5]. It is highly recommended for **early collection grinding**, as it allows you to gather large amounts of ores like gold or diamonds very quickly [3, 6]. It is also later used for specific mechanics, such as stunning Kuudra [7].
*   **Mining Speed Boost (Active Ability):** Provides a massive temporary boost to your mining speed. While Pickobulus is often preferred for raw collection, this ability is useful for breaking through high-durability blocks like Mithril or Titanium early on [1].

### HOTM Tier 3
*   **Efficient Miner:** This node gives you a percentage chance to mine adjacent blocks of the same type when you break an ore [8]. It is a massive "quality of life" upgrade that significantly speeds up collection rates for ores in the Dwarven Mines and Crystal Hollows [8, 9].
*   **Luck of the Cave:** Provides a chance to trigger rare events or find rare items while mining. While useful, it is generally considered lower priority than raw Fortune or Speed for players specifically looking to progress their collections or earn money [1].

### Summary of Early Value
For the most immediate impact, players should prioritize **Mining Fortune I** and the **Pickobulus** ability [3]. These nodes directly translate to more resources per minute, which is essential for crafting basic mining gear and unlocking more advanced areas like the **Deep Caverns** and **Dwarven Mines** [6, 10]. Once you reach Tier 3, **Efficient Miner** becomes a top priority for its ability to clear multiple ore blocks simultaneously [8, 9].

---

## Q53: What HOTM nodes should players unlock at HOTM 4-6? What changes at these tiers and what is the priority order?

**Answer:**

At Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) levels 4 through 6, your progression shifts from the basic Dwarven Mines into the **Crystal Hollows** and advanced powder-gathering strategies [1, 2].

### **HOTM Tier 4: The Crystal Hollows Transition**
Reaching HOTM 4 is a major milestone because it **unlocks access to the Crystal Hollows**, a much larger mining area with its own unique progression and loot [1, 2].

*   **Key Node to Unlock:** **Sky Mall**. This node provides a different random buff every SkyBlock day, such as increased Mining Speed, 10x Golden/Diamond Goblin spawn rates, or 5x more Titanium [3-5].
*   **Secondary Node:** **Mining Madness**. This provides a flat buff to both Mining Fortune and Mining Speed for a limited time after starting a mining session, which is useful for general efficiency.
*   **Priority Order:** Focus on **Sky Mall** first to take advantage of its powerful 10x Goblin buffs, which are currently essential for farming rare items like **Pixie Dust** and **Shield Dust** during the Year of the Witch event [4, 6].

### **HOTM Tier 5: Efficiency and Forge Upgrades**
At Tier 5, your focus shifts toward maximizing the rewards from your daily tasks and unlocking higher-tier equipment.

*   **Forge Change:** Reaching HOTM 5 allows you to upgrade your **Mithril Lantern** into the **Titanium Lantern**, a critical deployable for mining setups [7-9].
*   **Key Node to Unlock:** **Daily Powder**. This node significantly increases the amount of Mithril and Gemstone powder you receive from your first few completed commissions each day [1, 10].
*   **Secondary Node:** **Titanium Insanity**. This increases your chances of spawning Titanium, which helps speed up Dwarven Mines commissions.
*   **Priority Order:** Prioritize **Daily Powder**. Mining progression in 2026 relies heavily on the thousands of hours' worth of powder needed for late-game nodes, and this is the most efficient passive way to accumulate it [1, 11].

### **HOTM Tier 6: Preparing for the Powder Grind**
HOTM 6 contains nodes that are vital for "Powder Mining," the meta-strategy for players preparing for the hyper-expensive late-game tiers.

*   **Key Node to Unlock:** **Mole**. This node is the top priority. It allows you to clear multiple blocks at once when mining in the Crystal Hollows, which is necessary for spawning treasure chests quickly to maximize powder gains [12, 13].
*   **Secondary Node:** **Professional**. This provides a significant increase in Mining Speed for specific ores and is crucial if you are struggling to "insta-mine" materials like Gold or Diamonds in the Mines of Divan [14, 15].
*   **Priority Order:** **Mole** should be your absolute priority to prepare for the massive powder requirements of HOTM 7 and 10 [12].

### **Contextual Priority: Mining Abilities**
Regardless of your tier, you should select and prioritize these abilities based on your current activity:
*   **Maniac Miner:** Recommended for specialized mining tasks like Diamond or Endstone mining because it heavily drains mana to provide speed [15-17].
*   **Picobolus (Pokbolus):** Essential for gold farming as it breaks a massive amount of ores at once [18, 19]. It is also the meta requirement for **stunning Kuudra** in the Burning tier and above [20, 21].
*   **Mining Speed Boost:** Generally the best for gemstone or ruby mining where "ticks" and speed are more valuable than area damage [14].

**Tip:** Always complete your **first 4 commissions every day**, as they provide a massive bonus to HOTM XP, regardless of your current tier [2, 10].

---

## Q54: What HOTM nodes should players unlock at HOTM 7-10? What are the most powerful T7-T10 nodes and what do they unlock?

**Answer:**

Based on the sources, reaching Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) tiers 7 through 10 is essential for maximizing mining efficiency, particularly for gemstones and specialized materials in the Glacite Tunnels.

### Most Powerful T7–T10 Nodes
The most significant high-tier nodes focus on raw stat increases and specialized gemstone buffs:

*   **Pristine (HOTM 10):** This is considered one of the most powerful late-game nodes, providing a flat **+1 Pristine** [1]. This stat is critical for gemstone mining as it increases the chance to drop higher-tier gems [1, 2].
*   **Front Loader:** This perk provides a significant burst of **+150 Mining Fortune** and **+250 Mining Speed** for the first 2,500 blocks mined in a session [1].
*   **Gem Lover & Professional:** These are essential for gemstone scaling, providing extra mining fortune and speed specifically for gems [1].
*   **Block Head & Old School:** These nodes are specialized for different mining types. **Block Head** provides +100 Block Fortune (ideal for endstone), while **Old School** provides extra Ore Fortune (ideal for gold and diamonds) [3-5].
*   **Powder Buff (HOTM 7):** While not a new node in 2026, it remains a priority at Tier 7 to maximize the efficiency of powder grinding in the Crystal Hollows and Glacite Tunnels [6, 7].

### Essential Abilities
Depending on your activity, you should toggle between these powerful active abilities:

*   **Mining Speed Boost:** Grants a massive **250% to 300% increase in Mining Speed** (the higher value requires a Blue Goblin Egg) [1]. It is the standard for gemstone and ore mining [1, 8].
*   **Maniac Miner:** Consumes mana to grant a high degree of speed; it is the preferred ability for instamining blocks like Endstone or Gold in the Mines of Divan [4, 9, 10].
*   **Pickobulus:** This ability is specifically required for players performing the "stunner" role in **Burning Tier Kuudra**, as it is used to disrupt the boss [11, 12].

### Strategic Unlocks and Forge Progression
Advancing through T7–T10 also unlocks the ability to craft powerful mining deployables in the Forge:

*   **HOTM 8 Unlocks:** The ability to upgrade the Titanium Lantern into the **Glacite Lantern** [13, 14].
*   **HOTM 10 Unlocks:** The final upgrade for mining deployables, the **Will-o-wisp** [13, 14]. This item is extremely powerful, granting up to 10 players within 30 blocks (or all players in a mineshaft) **+100 Mining Speed, +25 Mining Fortune, +20 Heat Resistance, +10 Cold Resistance, and +2.5 Gemstone Spread** [15].

### Notable Changes to High-Tier Perks
The **Miner's Blessing** perk was recently reworked. Previously, it granted +30 Magic Find on mining islands; it now grants **+1 extra drop from the Crystal Nucleus**, making it a buff for Alloy and Scatha pet grinding [16, 17].

## Q55: What are HOTM Token abilities (Peak of the Mountain, etc.) and which should players unlock first?

**Answer:**

In the Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) system, **Tokens of the Mountain** are used to unlock various active abilities and passive perks that enhance mining efficiency. You earn these tokens by increasing your HOTM Tier through **commissions** and daily mining activities in the Dwarven Mines [1, 2].

### **Core HOTM Active Abilities**
Active abilities are powerful skills that you must trigger manually and usually have a cooldown.

*   **Pickobulus:** This ability allows you to "blow a bunch of blocks up" in a radius [3, 4]. It is highly recommended for tasks like the **Gold Ingot Collector** goal because it harvests many blocks at once [5-7]. It is also a strict requirement if you want to play the "stunner" role in **Burning Kuudra** [3, 4].
*   **Mining Speed Boost:** Grants a massive temporary increase to your Mining Speed (250%, or 300% if utilizing specific items like the Blue Goblin Egg) [8].
*   **Maniac Miner:** An endgame ability often used for highly efficient mining routes, such as for diamonds, coal, or gold [9-11]. Unlike other abilities, it consumes **mana** as fuel [9, 12].

### **Key Passive Perks**
Passives provide permanent stat boosts to your mining profile.

*   **Mining Fortune & Mining Speed:** These are the fundamental perks of the tree [7, 13]. Mining Fortune is particularly critical because it increases the number of resources dropped per block [7].
*   **Sky Mall:** Provides various rotating daily buffs, such as a **10x spawn rate** for Golden and Diamond Goblins, which is excellent for completing commissions or grinding specific drops [14, 15].
*   **Professional and Gem Lover:** Targeted perks that increase Mining Speed and Fortune specifically for **gemstones** [8].
*   **Block Head and Old School:** Specialized fortune perks—**Block Head** increases fortune for basic blocks (like Endstone), while **Old School** increases it for ores (like Gold) [11, 16].
*   **Miner's Blessing:** Recently reworked to grant an extra drop from the **Crystal Nucleus** instead of its previous Magic Find bonus [17].
*   **Gifts from the Departed & Dead Man's Chest:** Newer perks related to looting **Frozen Corpses** in the Glacite Tunnels [18, 19].

### **Which Should You Unlock First?**
Based on player guides and meta strategies for 2026, you should prioritize your unlocks in this order:

1.  **Mining Fortune and Mining Speed:** For beginners, these are the most important. You should aim to get your **Mining Fortune** as high as possible first to maximize your resource gain from every block broken [7].
2.  **Reach HOTM Tier 4:** Your primary early-game goal should be leveling your tree through commissions until you hit **HOTM 4**, which unlocks the **Crystal Hollows**, a major area for progression and money-making [20, 21].
3.  **Pickobulus:** Unlock this early as your first active ability. It is versatile for both collection-based goals and specialized combat like Kuudra [3, 7].
4.  **Sky Mall:** This is a high-value unlock because its rotating buffs can significantly speed up your commission progress and goblin-related drops [14, 15].
5.  **Professional / Gem Lover:** Once you reach the **Crystal Hollows** and begin mining gemstones (specifically Ruby in the Magma Fields), you should start investing in these gemstone-specific passives [8, 22].

## Q56: Explain how Mining powder works. What is Mithril powder, Gemstone powder, Glacite powder, and how does each get spent?

**Answer:**

In Hypixel SkyBlock, **Mining powder** functions as a specialized currency used to progress through the **Heart of the Mountain (HotM)**, which is the mining skill tree [1]. These powders are spent to unlock and level up **perks** that provide permanent stat boosts, such as Mining Speed, Mining Fortune, and Pristine [1-3].

### **Mithril Powder**
*   **What it is:** The primary powder earned within the **Dwarven Mines** [4].
*   **How to obtain it:** It is rewarded for completing **commissions**, participating in **mining events** (which can grant up to 20,000 powder), and completing daily tasks from **Fetchur** and the **Puzzler** [1, 5, 6]. It also drops from certain mobs, such as **Ghosts** (when using a Mithril Golem pet), **Star Sentries**, and **Golden Goblins** (10,000 powder) [5, 7, 8].
*   **How it is spent:** Mithril powder is spent on the **base and mid-tier perks** of the Heart of the Mountain tree, including basic Mining Speed and Mining Fortune upgrades [1, 2].

### **Gemstone Powder**
*   **What it is:** A higher-tier powder associated with the **Crystal Hollows** [6].
*   **How to obtain it:** It is primarily earned while mining in the **Crystal Hollows** and by looting chests found there [6, 9]. It is also dropped in large quantities by **Diamond Goblins** (40,000 Mithril and Gemstone powder) [8, 10].
*   **How it is spent:** It is used to upgrade **advanced HotM perks**, such as **Pristine**, **Professional**, and **Gem Lover**, which are critical for maximizing efficiency when mining gemstones [3].

### **Glacite Powder**
*   **What it is:** The newest mining powder, introduced with the **Glacite Tunnels** and **Mineshafts** [11, 12].
*   **How to obtain it:** You can earn Glacite powder by looting **Frozen Corpses** (Lapis, Umber, Tungsten, and Vanguard tiers) in the Glacite Mineshafts [13, 14]. It is also a guaranteed drop from the **Littlefoot** mob, which yields **10,000 Glacite powder** per kill [15, 16]. Additionally, **Grandpa Wolf milestones** reward players with large amounts of this powder (up to 200,000) for looting corpses [14, 17].
*   **How it is spent:** Similar to other powders, it is used for mining progression within the Glacite-specific systems [14]. Your total accumulated Glacite powder is also a factor in **SkyBlock XP** progress for the Heart of the Mountain [8, 10].

---

## Q57: What are the best ways to farm Mining powder efficiently? Compare commissions vs. active mining.

**Answer:**

Efficiently farming Mining powder in 2026 involves a strategic combination of high-yield daily routines and intensive active mining sessions. The optimal method depends on whether you are prioritizing **Heart of the Mountain (HotM) XP** or raw **Mithril, Gemstone, or Glacite Powder** accumulation.

### **1. Daily Routines and NPC Tasks**
For consistent, low-effort progression, players should focus on daily bonuses which offer significantly higher efficiency per minute spent:
*   **Daily Commissions:** The **first four commissions completed each day** grant a substantial bonus to HotM XP, making them the most efficient way to progress your mining tree [1, 2].
*   **Daily Powder NPCs:** Interacting with **Fetchur** and the **Puzzler** in the Dwarven Mines provides a guaranteed **4,000 to 5,000 powder daily** for very little work [3, 4].
*   **Mining Events:** Participating in active mining events is highly recommended, as some can reward up to **20,000 powder at once** [3].

### **2. Active Powder Mining (Crystal Hollows)**
Once a player reaches **HotM 7**, active powder mining via "chest hunting" in the Crystal Hollows becomes the superior method for bulk accumulation [5]:
*   **Location Strategy:** Mining in the **Precursor Remnants** is cited as the best for both money and powder, yielding approximately **6.5 more powder per chest** than the Jungle [6]. The Jungle is more consistent for "Sludge Juice" but slightly less efficient for raw powder [7, 8].
*   **Equipment Optimization:** Use a **Fleet Jungle Pickaxe** for the fastest block breaking [7]. To maximize gains, swap to a high-tier drill (like a Ruby or Gemstone drill) equipped with a **Spicy Goblin Omelette** specifically when opening chests to receive a **5% to 20% powder bonus** [5, 7].

### **3. Specialized Combat-Mining Methods**
For late-game players with high combat stats, certain mobs provide alternative powder sources:
*   **Ghost Farming:** Killing Ghosts with a **MYTHIC Mithril Golem pet** (using the "Ghost Buster" perk) and **Sorrow Armor** is a viable high-level strategy for grinding Mithril Powder [9, 10].
*   **Elusive Goblins:** **Golden Goblins** reward 10,000 Mithril Powder, while **Diamond Goblins** provide a massive **40,000 Mithril and Gemstone Powder** upon death [11, 12].
*   **Glacite Powder:** In the Glacite Tunnels, the primary source is looting **Frozen Corpses** [13]. Players can also hunt the **Littlefoot** mob, which has a 100% chance to drop **10,000 Glacite Powder** [14, 15].

### **Commissions vs. Active Mining: Which is Better?**
*   **Commissions** are the definitive choice for **early-game progression** and leveling the HotM tree. They provide the necessary XP to unlock better perks and higher-tier zones like the Crystal Hollows [4, 16].
*   **Active Mining (Chest Hunting)** is the most efficient method for **bulk powder farming** once your HotM level is maxed. While commissions offer steady gains, they cannot compete with the raw volume of powder generated by opening dozens of chests per hour in the Precursor Remnants [5, 6].
*   **Glacite Shafting** is currently considered a very high-effort method, requiring approximately **40+ hours of dedicated powder grinding** to reach peak efficiency [17].

---

## Q58: What is the Glacite Tunnels area? How do you access it (HOTM level required), what do you do there, and how much XP/powder can players earn?

**Answer:**

The **Glacite Tunnels** is a late-game mining area introduced in update 0.20 [1]. It is a massive subterranean zone consisting of several locations, including the **Dwarven Base Camp**, **Grandpa Wolf's Cave**, the **Great Glacite Lake**, and various **Glacite Mineshafts** [2].

### **Access and Requirements**
The Glacite Tunnels is categorized as a **late-game mining area** [1]. While the sources do not explicitly state a single "unlock" level for entry, progression within the zone is tied to high **Heart of the Mountain (HOTM)** tiers. For example, upgrading the Titanium Lantern to a **Glacite Lantern** requires **HOTM 8**, and the final tier, the **Will-o-wisp**, requires **HOTM 10** [3-5]. Players often view reaching HOTM 10 as a primary goal for this area [6].

### **Activities in the Glacite Tunnels**
*   **Looting Frozen Corpses:** Players can find and loot different tiers of corpses (Lapis, Umber, Tungsten, and Vanguard) to obtain rare items like the **Shattered Locket** or gemstones [7-9].
*   **Grandpa Wolf Milestones:** Located at coordinates -56, 123, 332, Grandpa Wolf offers rewards based on the number of corpses you have looted. These rewards include SkyBlock XP, Glacite Powder, and a new talisman line [3, 9, 10].
*   **Exploring Glacite Mineshafts:** These are unique instances spawned within the tunnels. A rare variant called **Littlefoot's Den** was recently added [11-13].
*   **Hunting Littlefoot:** This creature has a 1% chance to replace any mob in standard mineshafts (or 100% in its den) [14-16]. It drops materials needed to upgrade the **Baby Yeti pet to Mythic** [11, 17, 18].
*   **Mining New Minerals:** The area features Glacite, Umber, and Tungsten collections and gemstones [1, 19].

### **XP and Powder Earnings**
Players can earn significant amounts of powder and combat experience through several methods:

| Source | XP/Powder Type | Amount |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Littlefoot Kill** | Combat XP | +3,998 XP [16, 20] |
| **Littlefoot Kill** | Glacite Powder | 10,000 Powder [16, 20] |
| **Lapis Corpse** | RNG Meter XP | 500 XP [7, 9, 21] |
| **Umber/Tungsten Corpse** | RNG Meter XP | 2,500 XP [7, 9, 21] |
| **Vanguard Corpse** | RNG Meter XP | 25,000 XP [7, 9, 21] |
| **Milestone 1 (Grandpa Wolf)**| Glacite Powder | 10,000 Powder [9, 10, 22] |
| **Max Milestone (Grandpa Wolf)**| Glacite Powder | 200,000 Powder [10, 22] |

Additionally, "Glacite Shafting" is considered a high-level money-making method that can yield roughly **60 million coins per hour**, though it requires a very expensive gear setup and an extensive powder grind (40+ hours) to be efficient [23].

## Q59: What are Glacite Commissions? How do they work, what XP do they give, and why are they considered essential for HOTM progression?

**Answer:**

**Glacite Commissions** are specialized mining tasks located in the **Glacite Tunnels**, a late-game mining area introduced in update 0.20 [1, 2]. They function as the primary mechanism for progressing through the higher tiers of the **Heart of the Mountain (HOTM)** system [3, 4].

### How Glacite Commissions Work
Players receive these assignments through the commission system, typically accessed via the King in the Dwarven Mines [4]. Each commission requires the completion of a specific objective within the Glacite Tunnels or Mineshafts, such as:
*   **Mob Hunting:** For example, the "Elusive Goblin Slayer" commission requires killing a Golden or Diamond Goblin [2].
*   **Resource Collection:** Mining specific ores or gemstones found within the frozen tunnels [1].
*   **Mineshaft Exploration:** Objectives tied to the randomly spawning Glacite Mineshafts, where unique mobs like **Littlefoot** reside [5, 6].

Players can typically hold two commission slots at once, though more can be unlocked as they progress through the HOTM tree [7, 8].

### Rewards and XP
Completing these commissions is the most efficient way to gain several essential mining resources:
*   **HOTM XP:** This is the core reward used to increase your Heart of the Mountain tier [3].
*   **Mithril and Gemstone Powder:** Used to upgrade various perks within the mining skill tree [3, 4].
*   **Daily Bonus:** The **first four commissions** completed each day provide a substantial **bonus to HOTM XP**, making consistent daily play the fastest way to level up [8, 9].

### Essential Role in HOTM Progression
Glacite Commissions are considered essential for several reasons:
*   **Main Progression Path:** They are described as the "fastest 'I'm actually progressing' path" for any player looking to climb the HOTM ladder [4].
*   **Unlocking New Content:** Higher tiers of HOTM are required to access advanced areas and features. For instance, the **Crystal Hollows** is locked until HOTM Tier 4 [8], and reaching **HOTM Tier 10** is a major milestone for endgame mining profiles [10].
*   **Stat Enhancement:** Leveling through these commissions grants **Tokens of the Mountain**, which are required to unlock powerful perks like increased Mining Speed, Mining Fortune, and specialized abilities like **Maniac Miner** [3, 11].
*   **Account QOL:** Pushing HOTM levels through commissions also contributes to overall **SkyBlock XP**, helping players increase their total SkyBlock Level [4, 12].

---

## Q60: What is the Crystal Hollows? How do players unlock it, what activities are there, and what is the best reason to go there?

**Answer:**

The **Crystal Hollows** is a specialized, late-game mining region designed for advanced progression and resource gathering [1, 2]. It is a vast area composed of different biomes, such as the **Jungle**, **Precursor Remnants**, **Goblin Burrows**, and **Magma Fields**, each containing unique resources and enemies [3-5].

### **Unlocking the Crystal Hollows**
To gain access to the Crystal Hollows, players must reach **Heart of the Mountain (HotM) Tier 4** [2, 6]. This is accomplished by earning HotM experience through the completion of **mining commissions** and daily activities like the Puzzler or Fetchur's requests within the Dwarven Mines [2, 7]. Once this milestone is reached, players can travel to the Hollows to continue their mining progression [2, 8].

### **Available Activities**
The Crystal Hollows offers a wide variety of gameplay loops centered around the mining skill:
*   **Gemstone Mining:** This is the primary activity, where players mine various types of gemstones (such as Ruby) to sell for profit or use in crafting [5, 9]. High **Pristine** stats are essential here to maximize the chance of getting better gemstone tiers [9, 10].
*   **Crystal Nucleus Runs:** Players search for specific crystals located in different zones to complete a "Nucleus Run" at the central **Crystal Nucleus**, which provides high-value rewards [4, 11].
*   **Powder Grinding:** Farming for **Mithril and Gemstone Powder** is often done here, specifically in the Jungle (where players also collect Sludge Juice) or the Precursor Remnants (where players hunt Automatons for parts) [3, 4, 12].
*   **Mob Hunting:** Players slay unique regional mobs for rare drops, including **Automatons** for electronic parts, **Sludges** for sludge juice, and **Thysts** [4, 13]. It is also the only location to hunt **Worms and Scathas** for rare pet drops [13, 14].
*   **Boss Encounters:** The area features unique bosses, such as **Bal**, who resides in the Magma Fields and drops items like the Bal pet [15, 16].
*   **Specialized Fishing:** Players can participate in both water and lava fishing to catch exclusive sea creatures like the **Abyssal Miner**, **Lava Blaze**, or **Flaming Worm** [17].

### **The Best Reason to Go**
The best reason to go to the Crystal Hollows is that it remains a **foundational money-making and progression path** for mid-to-late-game players [8]. It is the most effective location for obtaining **Gemstone and Mithril powder**, which are required to max out the Heart of the Mountain tree [3]. Additionally, highly optimized **gemstone mining** setups in the Hollows can generate significant hourly coin yields, while **Nucleus Runs** and **Scatha hunting** offer opportunities for high-value "RNG" drops [5, 8, 18].

---

## Q61: Explain gemstone mining in detail. What types of gemstones are there, which are most profitable, and how does the Pristine stat affect drops?

**Answer:**

Gemstone mining is a high-level progression loop within the Mining skill where players break gemstone nodes to obtain valuable gems used for stat upgrades or profit [1, 2]. In early 2026, this system is centered in the **Crystal Hollows** and the **Glacite Tunnels** [3-5].

### Types of Gemstones
The ecosystem features several varieties of gemstones, each serving different stat-boosting purposes:
*   **Standard Gemstones:** These include **Ruby, Topaz, Jasper, Jade, Sapphire, Amber, Amethyst, and Opal** [6, 7].
*   **Glacite Tunnels Gemstones:** Introduced in the 2026 updates, these high-tier gems are primarily found in **Glacite Mineshafts** and include **Onyx, Citrine, Peridot, and Aquamarine** [8-10].
*   **Specialty Crystals:** Some variants like **Onyx, Citrine, and Peridot Crystals** are found as rare rewards from higher-tier containers like **Vanguard Corpses** [8, 11].

### Profitability and Meta Methods
As of Q1 2026, the profitability of gemstone mining is defined by "active" participation rather than passive generation [12].
*   **Glacite Shafting:** This is currently one of the most profitable methods in the game, yielding approximately **60 million coins per hour**. However, it is an elite activity requiring a setup worth roughly 6 billion coins and over 40 hours of "powder grinding" for preparation [13, 14].
*   **Ruby Mining:** Focused in the **Magma Fields**, this remains a consistent late-game method. With a hypermaxed setup, players can generate **25 to 30 million coins per hour** [15, 16]. Ruby is often preferred because it is considered the quickest gemstone to mine [17].
*   **Diamond and Gold Alternatives:** While not exclusively gemstone-focused, **Diamond Mining** in the Dwarven Mines can reach up to **115 million coins per hour** for the most optimized players, though it is considered significantly more difficult skill-wise [18, 19].

### The Pristine Stat and Drop Mechanics
The **Pristine** stat is the primary factor in determining the quality and quantity of gemstone drops.
*   **Function:** Pristine increases the chance for gemstones to drop in higher rarities (such as **Fine, Flawless, or Perfect**) when mined [9, 17, 20].
*   **Key Sources:**
    *   **Heart of the Mountain (HotM):** A dedicated **Pristine perk** is available at **HotM Tier 10**, granting a flat +1 Pristine [17].
    *   **Bal Pet:** While in the Magma Fields, the Bal pet provides a significant bonus of **+3 Pristine**, making it the meta choice for Ruby mining [21].
    *   **Gear:** High-end armor sets like the **Armor of Divan** are essential for maxing out Pristine, alongside specialized equipment like the **Pendant of Divan**, which was updated to include **Gemstone Spread** bonuses [22, 23].
*   **Synergy:** Players often use **Maniac Miner** as their primary ability and stack mining speed and fortune to maximize the number of rolls the Pristine stat gets per hour [17, 24]. For example, a setup might utilize the **Littlefoot Shard**, which provides **Gemstone Spread** based on your Cold Resistance, allowing you to mine multiple blocks simultaneously in Glacite Mineshafts [14, 25].

## Q62: What is the best gemstone mining setup? What armor, drill, pets, and HOTM nodes are needed for maximum coins/hr?

**Answer:**

For a hyper-maxed gemstone mining setup in early 2026, the meta centers on maximizing **Pristine**, **Mining Fortune**, and **Mining Speed** through a combination of Divan gear and new 2026 technical additions like mining lanterns and attributes.

### **1. Best Armor**
The definitive armor for gemstone mining remains a **full set of Jaded Armor of Divan** [1]. 
*   **Gemstones:** Each piece must be fitted with **Perfect Gemstones** (specifically Topaz for Pristine, Amber for Mining Speed, and Jade for Mining Fortune) [1, 2].
*   **Alternative (Ore Mining):** If you are mining ores like Diamonds for coins, a **3/4 Glossy Mineral Armor** set with one piece of **Divan** is preferred to utilize **Mining Spread** while maintaining high fortune [3].

### **2. Best Drill and Parts**
The apex tool is the **Divan's Drill** [4, 5]. To reach maximum efficiency, it should be equipped with the following parts:
*   **Engine:** **Amber-Polished Drill Engine** for maximum mining speed [6, 7].
*   **Fuel Tank:** **Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank** [4].
*   **Upgrade Module:** **Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette** is the standard for hyper-maxed setups, though **Starfall Seasoning** is also a top-tier choice [6, 8].
*   **Drill Reforge:** **Lustrous** is typically used for general mining, while **Fleet** is used when specific speed breakpoints are needed for instamining [9, 10].
*   **Double Drill Tech:** High-end players often use a **double drill setup**, keeping a secondary drill with a **Blue Goblin Egg** to swap to for the Mining Speed Boost ability [1, 11].

### **3. Best Pets**
*   **Scatha (Legendary/Mythic):** The overall best pet for gemstone mining due to its mining fortune and ability to find treasure [11].
*   **Bal (Legendary):** **Mandatory for Ruby mining** or any mining within the **Magma Fields**. Its ability provides a massive **+3 Pristine** buff and increases your stats by 15% while in the fields [11, 12].
*   **Goblin Pet:** Used in specific scenarios (like the Mines of Divan) for increased **Mining Spread** and ore fortune [11, 13].

### **4. Key Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) Nodes**
With the expansion of the Heart of the Mountain to Tier 10, several nodes are critical for maximum yield [1]:
*   **Pristine Perk (HOTM 10):** Grants an additional +1 Pristine, which is vital for gemstone profits [1].
*   **Professional:** Specifically increases mining speed on gemstones [1].
*   **Gem Lover:** Increases gemstone fortune [1].
*   **Front Loader:** Provides a massive temporary buff to mining fortune (+150) and speed (+250) for the first 2,500 blocks mined in a session [1].
*   **Mining Speed & Fortune:** Both base nodes should be maxed [1].
*   **Ability:** **Mining Speed Boost** remains the superior choice for gemstone yield [1].

### **5. Essential 2026 Additions**
*   **Mining Lanterns:** The **Will-o-wisp** is a new proximity-based deployable that grants players within 30 blocks **+25 Mining Fortune**, **+100 Mining Speed**, and **+2.5 Gemstone Spread** [14].
*   **Equipment Attributes:** The **Littlefoot Shard** attribute is highly valued for high-end setups; it grants **+0.5 Gemstone Spread** for every 20-29 Cold Resistance while in Glacite Mineshafts [15, 16].
*   **Talisman Buffs:** The **Pendant of Divan** now features a "Legacy" Tiered Bonus that grants **+2.5 Gemstone Spread** [14, 17].

### **Estimated Yield (Coins/hr)**
*   **Ruby Mining (Magma Fields):** A hyper-maxed setup earns approximately **25M–30M coins/hr** [5].
*   **Glacite Shafting:** This newer 2026 method is currently more profitable, yielding around **60M coins/hr**, though it requires a specialized gear setup worth roughly **6 billion coins** [18].
*   **Diamond Mining:** In highly optimized routes using **Etherwarp** and **Glossy Mineral Armor**, players can reach upwards of **115M coins/hr**, though this is significantly more difficult to execute than standard gemstone mining [19].

## Q63: What is Divan's Armor and Divan's Drill? How are they obtained, what do they cost, and are they worth the investment?

**Answer:**

**Armor of Divan** and **Divan's Drill** are the absolute pinnacle of mining equipment in Hypixel SkyBlock, required for players who wish to "hypermax" their mining efficiency and profit [1-3].

### **What are they?**
*   **Armor of Divan:** This is the premier endgame mining armor set, categorized under Dwarven Mines gear [4]. It is prized for its high number of gemstone slots, allowing players to significantly boost stats like Mining Fortune, Mining Speed, and Pristine by using perfect gemstones [2, 5, 6].
*   **Divan's Drill:** This is the highest tier of Titanium Drill and the overall best mining tool in the game [1]. It is capable of reaching mining speeds high enough to "instamine" gold in the Mines of Divan, which is a requirement for maximizing certain gold-mining strategies [2].

### **How are they obtained?**
*   **Armor of Divan:** The armor is part of the Dwarven Mines and Glacite Tunnels progression [4]. While the specific recipe is not fully detailed in these sources, it is listed alongside other gear like Glacite and Sorrow armor that is forged or crafted from high-tier mining materials [4].
*   **Divan's Drill:** This drill is the final upgrade in the Titanium Drill line (starting from the DR-X355) [1]. Its creation is centered around the **Divan Alloy**, an extremely rare and expensive material [7, 8].

### **What do they cost?**
Investing in a full Divan setup is one of the most expensive undertakings in the game:
*   **The Drill:** The **Divan Alloy** alone has been valued at approximately **850 million coins** at a low point [8].
*   **Full Gear Setup:** A hypermaxed mining setup—which includes Divan's armor, the drill, and the necessary gemstones and powder—is estimated to cost around **6 billion coins** [9].
*   **Upgrades:** Individual pieces, such as a Jaded Helmet of Divan with perfect gemstones, represent significant additional costs [5].

### **Are they worth the investment?**
Whether these items are "worth it" depends on your commitment to the mining skill:
*   **High Profit Potential:** With a maxed setup including this gear, players can earn **60 million coins per hour** through Glacite Shafting [9]. Optimized diamond mining can yield anywhere from **30 million to 150 million coins per hour**, though this requires immense player skill and "ether warp" efficiency [10].
*   **Comparison to Other Skills:** Mining is significantly more expensive to start than farming but generally offers a higher ceiling for hourly profit [11]. For example, "hypermaxed" ruby mining yields roughly **25–30 million coins per hour** [2].
*   **Conclusion:** If you intend to make mining your primary source of active income, the investment is necessary to reach the "hypermax potential" required to compete with the game's best money-making methods [3, 10]. However, for casual players, the multi-billion coin entry fee may take hundreds of hours of active mining to pay for itself [9, 11].

## Q64: Compare income from gemstone mining at HOTM 5, HOTM 7, and HOTM 10 setups. How much do earnings increase at each stage?

**Answer:**

Based on the provided sources, gemstone mining income scales significantly with Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) levels, peaking at **HOTM 10** with the introduction of new perks and specialized endgame content like Glacite Shafting.

### **Income and Setup Comparison**

#### **HOTM 5: The Transitional Stage**
At this stage, players are typically moving out of early-game mining (coal, iron, and gold) and into the **Deep Caverns and Crystal Hollows** [1, 2]. 
*   **Setup:** Players often use **Glacite or Mineral armor** paired with basic drills or high-tier pickaxes like the Fractured Mithril Pickaxe [3, 4]. At HOTM 5, players unlock the **Titanium Lantern** mining deployable through the Forge [5, 6].
*   **Earnings:** True gemstone mining is rarely efficient here due to low "Mining Speed" and "Mining Fortune." Casual mining for basic ores like diamonds or gold at this stage yields approximately **19M to 21M coins per hour** [7, 8].

#### **HOTM 7: The Powder Gate**
HOTM 7 is a critical breakpoint where players shift their focus to **Powder Mining** in the Crystal Hollows to prepare for high-end gemstone setups [9].
*   **Setup:** Recommended gear includes a **Ruby, Gemstone, or Jasper drill** often equipped with a **Spicy Goblin Omelette** [9]. Players must grind both Mithril and Gemstone powder (targeting 4M/4M or higher) to make gemstone mining viable [7, 10].
*   **Earnings:** Casual gemstone mining (such as Ruby mining in the Magma Fields) with a non-hypermaxed setup yields roughly **20M coins per hour** [11].

#### **HOTM 10: The Hypermax Meta**
HOTM 10 represents the current endgame meta for mining in 2026, introducing powerful perks that drastically increase yield [10].
*   **Setup:** Setup requirements are extremely high, often costing around **6 billion coins** [10]. Key components include:
    *   **Pristine Perk:** Unlocked at HOTM 10, providing an additional **+1 Pristine** [12].
    *   **Will-o-wisp:** A high-tier deployable unlocked at HOTM 10 that grants **+100 Mining Speed, +25 Mining Fortune, and +2.5 Gemstone Spread** to players within range [5, 13].
    *   **Armor/Drills:** Full Divan's Armor with perfect gemstones and a maxed Divan's Drill (DR-X655) [11, 14].
*   **Earnings:** Hypermaxed Ruby mining can reach **25M to 30M coins per hour** [11]. However, the most lucrative method at this stage is **Glacite Shafting**, which generates approximately **60M coins per hour** [10].

---

### **Earnings Increase Summary**

| Stage Transition | Primary Income Shift | Estimated Increase |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **HOTM 5 → HOTM 7** | Shift from basic ores to entry-level gemstone mining. | **Minimal Raw Coin Increase** (Focus is on powder accumulation) [9]. |
| **HOTM 7 → HOTM 10** | Shift from casual Ruby mining to hypermaxed Glacite Shafting. | **~200% Increase** (from 20M/hr to 60M/hr) [10, 11]. |

**Key Driver of Growth:** The jump between HOTM 7 and HOTM 10 is driven by the **Pristine perk** and the **Will-o-wisp deployable**, which provide the numerical scaling necessary to transition from casual mid-game mining to the elite "Glacite Shafting" meta [10, 12, 15].

## Q65: What are the best mining pets and when should players switch between them? Compare Scatha, Mole, Glacite Golem, Bat, Blue Whale.

**Answer:**

The selection of the best mining pet depends heavily on your specific activity, whether it be maximizing raw gemstone yields, grinding powder, or ensuring survivability in dangerous zones.

### **Best Mining Pets and Selection Criteria**

*   **Scatha:** This is widely considered the **best pet for high-end mining**, specifically for activities like **diamond mining** in the Dwarven Mines [1]. It is utilized in optimized mining routes to maximize profit per hour, such as during three-veined routes [2]. Scatha drops are also slated for rate buffs as part of the Magic Find revamp to make them more obtainable [3, 4].
*   **Mole:** Following its rework in the Magic Find update, the Mole pet has a highly specialized niche [3]. While it lost its base Magic Find, its **"Magnetic Nose" perk now grants +0.1–10 Magic Find specifically on Automatons**, making it the ideal switch when farming the Precursor Remnants for parts or rare drops [3, 5].
*   **Bat:** The Bat pet is primarily used for **reducing ability cooldowns** [2]. It is an essential switch for players using the **Maniac Miner** ability on active mining routes, as a level 100 Bat significantly decreases the downtime between ability uses [2].
*   **Blue Whale:** Although not a traditional mining pet, the Blue Whale is a **top-tier survivability pet** [6, 7]. Players should switch to it when mining in high-pressure environments where they might be "squishy" or under attack, such as when dealing with aggressive mobs in Glacite Mineshafts [7]. It has largely replaced the Baby Yeti in the defensive meta after the Yeti's strength-to-defense perk was removed [7].
*   **Glacite Golem:** The provided sources **do not contain specific stats or meta-analysis for a "Glacite Golem" pet**; however, they do detail the **Mithril Golem**, which has been reworked to focus on **Ghost farming** [8, 9]. Its "Ghost Buster" perk grants a 5% chance to drop 50–5,000 mithril powder when killing a Ghost, making it the preferred choice for players grinding powder in the Mist [3, 5].

### **When to Switch Between Pets**

| **Switch To...** | **When...** |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Scatha** | You are doing **Diamond Mining** or max-efficiency gemstone routes [1]. |
| **Mole** | You are farming **Automatons** in the Precursor Remnants [3, 5]. |
| **Bat** | You rely on **Maniac Miner** intelligence-based abilities and need faster cooldowns [2]. |
| **Blue Whale** | You are in a **dangerous mining zone** (like Glacite Mineshafts) and need high EHP to survive mobs [6, 7]. |
| **Endermite** | You are **Endstone Mining** to take advantage of the 100% chance to drop an extra block [10]. |
| **Goblin Pet** | You are **Gold Mining** in the Mines of Divan for the mining spread and ore fortune bonuses [11, 12]. |
| **Mithril Golem** | You are **Ghost Farming** in the Dwarven Mines to maximize Mithril Powder gains [8]. |

**Player Efficiency** remains the most deterministic factor in your mining rates regardless of the pet used, as stronger setups allow you to roll for rare drops and clear blocks more frequently [13, 14]. For instance, combining an **Etherwarp Aspect of the Void** with a high-end mining pet like the Scatha is recommended for the most optimized diamond mining yields [1].

## Q66: What mining enchantments are most important? List priority order for armor and tools.

**Answer:**

Based on the provided sources, the most important mining enchantments and their priority for progression are as follows:

### **Top Priority Mining Enchantments (Tools)**

For mining tools (pickaxes and drills), efficiency and yield are the primary focuses.

1.  **Efficiency (V to X):** This is the highest priority enchantment as it directly increases your mining speed, allowing you to break blocks faster [1-3]. Efficiency V is considered the baseline for a tool to "actually feel good" [4].
2.  **Fortune (III to IV):** Directly increases the number of items dropped from ores and blocks [4, 5]. It is essential for maximizing collection progress and profit [4, 5].
3.  **Pristine (I to V):** This is the single most important enchantment specifically for **gemstone mining** [6, 7]. It provides a chance to drop higher-rarity gemstones, which is the primary driver of gemstone profits [7, 8].
4.  **Looting (V):** Following the **2026 Magic Find Revolution**, Looting has been reworked and can now be applied to pickaxes [9, 10]. It provides a flat increase to **Magic Find** (up to +25 at max level), which is now vital for dropping rare items like the Scatha pet or Alloy [9, 10].
5.  **Experience (III):** Increases the amount of experience orbs dropped while mining, which is helpful for maintaining levels for other enchantments or utility [4, 11].
6.  **Compact (I to X):** While not explicitly ranked in a list, it is a standard mining enchantment that gives a small chance to drop an enchanted version of the block you are mining, saving time on crafting and increasing profit.

### **Mining Armor Priorities**

Mining armor progression relies less on specific mining-only enchantments and more on **survivability enchants, reforges, and gemstones**.

1.  **Protection V and Growth V:** These are the top priorities for any mining set to ensure you aren't "one-tapped" by mobs in zones like the Deep Caverns or the Great Ice Wall [12-14].
2.  **Gemstone Slots (Pristine/Fortune/Speed):** While technically a mechanic rather than an enchantment, unlocking and filling gemstone slots with **Perfect gemstones** (especially Topaz for Pristine and Amber for Mining Speed) is the most critical upgrade for high-end mining sets like Divan or Glossy Mineral [7, 15].
3.  **Reforges (Jaded):** The **Jaded** reforge is the primary "enchant-like" upgrade for armor, providing significant boosts to Mining Fortune and Mining Speed [15].
4.  **Rejuvenate V:** Highly recommended for any armor set used in dangerous areas to improve health regeneration and general tankiness [16].

### **Priority Summary Table**

| Priority | Tools (Pickaxes/Drills) | Armor Sets |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1 (Essential)** | **Efficiency** (Speed) [3] | **Growth/Protection** (Survivability) [14] |
| **2 (Yield)** | **Fortune** (Quantity) [4] | **Gemstone Slots** (Pristine/Fortune) [7] |
| **3 (Gemstones)** | **Pristine** (Gemstone rarity) [7] | **Reforges** (Jaded reforge) [15] |
| **4 (Rare Drops)** | **Looting** (Magic Find) [9, 10] | **Rejuvenate** (Sustain) [16] |
| **5 (QoL)** | **Experience** (Levels) [4] | **Hearth/Cold Resist Attributes** [17, 18] |

**Note on Modern Changes (2026):**
*   **Sunder** has been removed from the game; its functionality for axes was merged into the **Harvesting** enchantment for simplicity [19].
*   **Reforge Anvils** have been discontinued. All reforging and enchantment applications now occur in standard anvils [20, 21].
*   The **Feat** enchantment is a new addition mentioned in recent updates, though it is primarily focused on farming tools rather than standard mining [22, 23].

## Q67: What is the best early mining income method for players at HOTM 1-3 who can't do gemstone mining yet?

**Answer:**

For players at **Heart of the Mountain (HOTM) 1-3** who cannot yet access gemstone mining in the Crystal Hollows (which requires HOTM 4), there are several highly effective mining income methods centered in the Dwarven Mines and The End [1, 2].

### **1. Coal Mining in the Dwarven Mines (~37M/hr)**
According to recent testing, **coal mining** is one of the most profitable methods for early-to-mid-game players, yielding approximately **37 million coins per hour** [3, 4].
*   **Setup:** This method utilizes the **Heated reforge** on a mining tool, which provides additional mining fortune and speed based on your "Heat" level [3]. 
*   **Strategy:** Players typically build up their Heat by standing in lava until it reaches high levels (e.g., 85+) before starting a coal route [3]. Using a **Filet of Fortune** and **Glossy Mineral armor** allows you to mine multiple blocks at once, significantly increasing your yield [3, 5].
*   **Limitation:** This route can be competitive, as nodes may be mined out by other players or blocked by structures [4].

### **2. Diamond Mining in the Dwarven Mines (~21M/hr)**
Mining **diamond veins** within the Dwarven Mines is a reliable alternative that can generate about **21.2 million coins per hour** [3, 6].
*   **Setup:** Use **Glossy Mineral armor** (or standard Mineral armor) to take advantage of mining spread [5, 6].
*   **Strategy:** Use the **Maniac Miner** ability to increase your mining speed [7]. This ability consumes mana, so it is often paired with the **Sighted** power stone or intelligence-heavy reforges to ensure you don't run out of "fuel" [7, 8].

### **3. Endstone Mining (~20-25M/hr)**
If you have reached **Combat Level 12**, you can mine endstone in The End [9]. This method can produce **20 to 30 million coins per hour** depending on your optimizations [10, 11].
*   **Setup:** The **Endermite pet** is mandatory for this method because one of its perks grants a chance to drop double blocks, effectively doubling your rates [12]. 
*   **Strategy:** Use **Maniac Miner** and focus on **Block Fortune** (such as the Blockhead perk) because endstone is considered a block rather than an ore [13].

### **4. Low-Requirement Methods (~10M/hr)**
*   **Frozen Treasure Routes:** For players with zero gear requirements, running routes to find **frozen treasures** in the Glacite Tunnels can yield a steady **10 million coins per hour** [14].
*   **Glacite Walker Farming:** While often used for Combat XP, Glacite Walkers are killed with pickaxes and drop **Glacite Jewels** and armor pieces that sell for a decent profit early on [15, 16].

### **Recommended Early Gear for HOTM 1-3**
*   **Tool:** The **Fractured Mithril Pickaxe** (purchased from Bubu) is the standard starter upgrade for the Dwarven Mines [17].
*   **Armor:** **Glacite Armor** is highly recommended for its defense and mining stats in the early game [16, 18]. **Mineral Armor** is the gold standard for non-gemstone mining because it allows you to mine multiple adjacent blocks at once [5, 10].
*   **Pet:** The **Bee Pet** is an excellent budget option that provides early mining fortune [19]. For Endstone specifically, the **Endermite Pet** is the best choice [12].
