## Q68: Explain Mithril mining. How much does it earn, when is it worth doing, and what setup is best?

**Answer:**

**Mithril mining** is a specialized progression-based activity centered in the **Dwarven Mines**, an area unlocked once you reach **Mining Level 12** and provide 15 enchanted mining materials to the NPC Rhys [1, 2]. The system is built around the **Heart of the Mountain (HotM)**, a tiered skill tree where players earn **HotM XP** and **Mithril Powder** to upgrade perks such as Mining Speed and Mining Fortune [3]. Progress is primarily driven by **Commissions**, which are specific tasks assigned by the King that reward significant XP and powder [3, 4].

### **Earnings and Yields**
The income from mining in the Dwarven Mines and related areas varies significantly based on your gear and target material:
*   **Casual Ore Mining:** Casual diamond or gold mining in the mines can generate between **19 million and 22 million coins per hour** [5-7].
*   **Optimized Ore Mining:** With hyper-optimized setups, such as instamining gold or perfect diamond routes, rates can increase to **30 million coins per hour** or more [6, 8].
*   **Endgame Methods:** High-tier activities like **Glacite Shafting** can earn approximately **60 million coins per hour**, though this requires extremely expensive gear (roughly 6 billion coins) and over 40 hours of initial powder grinding [9].
*   **Ghost Farming:** Advanced players can earn **7.5 million to 15 million coins per hour** by killing Ghosts in the Mist, which also serves as a primary method for grinding Mithril Powder when using specific gear [9, 10].

### **When is it worth doing?**
*   **Early Progression:** Mining is essential in the early game to unlock the **Crystal Hollows** at **HotM Tier 4**, which is a prerequisite for more lucrative gemstone mining [4, 11].
*   **Daily Routine:** It is highly efficient to complete at least **four commissions daily**, as these provide a massive daily bonus to HotM XP [11].
*   **Mining Fiestas:** Mining is most profitable during **Mining Fiestas**, which occur in the 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th, and 10th months of the SkyBlock year and significantly boost drops and experience [12, 13].
*   **Powder Grinding:** It is worth focusing on Mithril mining whenever you need to upgrade HotM perks to increase your efficiency for other high-end money-making methods [3].

### **Recommended Setup**
The "best" setup depends on your stage of the game, but a top-tier configuration includes:
*   **Armor:** **Glossy Mineral Armor** is excellent for mining blocks like endstone or diamonds due to its spread ability, while a full set of **Armor of Divan** is the absolute best for maximum stats [14, 15]. **Sorrow Armor** is specifically recommended for Ghost farming to gain Mithril Powder [10].
*   **Tools:** The **Divan's Drill** is the pinnacle of mining equipment [16]. For those progressing, **Titanium Drills** (DR-X series) or **Mithril Drills** (SX-R326) are recommended, often enhanced with parts like the **Amber-Polished Drill Engine** and **Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank** [16, 17].
*   **Pets:** The **Scatha pet** is the best for general ore mining [18]. The **Mithril Golem** is essential for powder grinding, and the **Goblin pet** is useful for gold mining in the Mines of Divan [10, 19].
*   **Equipment:** A full **Titanium Equipment set** or the **Pendant of Divan** provides the best balance of speed and fortune while in the mines [20, 21].
*   **Perks:** The **Maniac Miner** active ability is highly recommended for maximizing block-breaking rates during timed mining routes [22, 23].

---

## Q69: What are the best ways to spend Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder?

**Answer:**

In Hypixel SkyBlock, Mithril, Gemstone, and Glacite Powders are primarily used to upgrade the various perks found in your **Heart of the Mountain (HotM)** skill tree [1]. As of early 2026, the strategic focus for spending these powders has shifted to accommodate the Glacite Tunnels and the rework of various stats like Magic Find.

### **Mithril Powder**
Mithril Powder is essential for baseline mining efficiency and specialized combat-mining tasks.
*   **Core Mining Stats:** Spend this powder to maximize **Mining Speed**, **Mining Fortune**, and **Mining Spread** [2, 3]. Mining Spread is particularly critical for maximizing yields in areas with dense ore veins, such as the Mines of Divan [3, 4].
*   **Mithril Golem Synergy:** Following the Magic Find rework, the **Mithril Golem pet** was updated with the **"Ghost Buster" perk**, which grants a chance to drop Mithril Powder when killing Ghosts [5]. Spending powder to optimize the **Sorrow Armor** tiered bonus—which now grants Magic Find and damage against Ghosts—can create a self-sustaining loop for grinding more powder [6].
*   **Maniac Miner:** This ability, which requires high intelligence to sustain, is a major target for Mithril Powder to enhance its effectiveness during active mining sessions [7, 8].

### **Gemstone Powder**
Gemstone Powder is the primary currency for high-end mining profit methods.
*   **High-Tier Perks:** Focus on spending Gemstone Powder on **Professional**, **Gem Lover**, and **Front Loader** [9]. These perks significantly boost Mining Speed and Fortune specifically for gemstones [9].
*   **Pristine:** This is arguably the most important stat for gemstone mining, and the **Pristine perk** (available at HotM 10) should be a priority for anyone mining Ruby or other high-value gems in the Magma Fields [9].
*   **Mining for Gold/Diamonds:** For late-game players, Gemstone Powder is used to max out perks that allow for efficient **Diamond mining** in the Dwarven Mines, which can net up to 30M coins per hour with a hypermaxed setup [10].

### **Glacite Powder**
Glacite Powder is specialized for the Glacite Tunnels and mining deployables.
*   **Glacite Tunnels Perks:** Spend this powder on newer perks such as **Dead Man's Chest** and **Gifts from the Departed** [11, 12]. Note that Dead Man's Chest was recently adjusted to provide a +0.5-25% bonus [11, 12].
*   **Mining Deployables:** Glacite Powder is relevant for players using the **Dwarven Lantern** line [11]. Upgrading these tools through the Forge (Mithril -> Titanium -> Glacite -> Will-o-wisp) provides massive proximity-based buffs, such as **+100 Mining Speed**, **+25 Mining Fortune**, and **+2.5 Gemstone Spread** for the final **Will-o-wisp** tier [13, 14].
*   **Organ Donor Rewards:** While not a direct "spend," collecting and utilizing Glacite Powder is linked to the **Grandpa Wolf milestones** in the Glacite Tunnels [15]. Reaching these milestones rewards the player with the **Organ Donor accessory line**, which provides Cold Resistance and helps locate unlooted Frozen Corpses [13, 16].

**Strategic Tip:** For general progression, analysts suggest using a **Fleet Jungle Pickaxe** to powder mine in the **Precursor Remnants** or **Jungle** [17]. The Precursor Remnants are cited as the most efficient area for both money (via parts) and total powder gain [18].

---

## Q70: How does the Mining skill level affect income? What unlocks at Mining 20, 30, 40, 50, 60?

**Answer:**

The Mining skill level significantly impacts your income by increasing your **Mining Fortune** and **Mining Speed**, which directly raises the yield and efficiency of resource gathering [1-3]. Higher Mining levels also contribute to your overall **SkyBlock Level**, which grants permanent bonuses such as **+1 Magic Find every 20 levels** [4, 5].

### **Mining Skill and Income Yields (Q1 2026 Meta)**
As of early 2026, specialized mining methods are among the highest-yielding activities in the game:
*   **Glacite Shafting:** Generates approximately **60 million coins per hour**. It requires a very high-end setup (valued at ~6B coins) and significant powder grinding [6, 7].
*   **Diamond Mining (Dwarven Mines):** Yields between **21.2 million and 30 million coins per hour** depending on efficiency and the use of ether warp to navigate routes [8, 9].
*   **Gold Mining (Mines of Divan):** Produces roughly **19.68 million to 22 million coins per hour**, with potential for **30 million+** if you have enough mining speed to "instamine" [2, 10, 11].
*   **Ruby Mining (Magma Fields):** Can net between **20 million and 30 million coins per hour** [11].
*   **Mining Fiestas:** These events can effectively **double your rates** (though lag may reduce the actual gain to around 1.5x for ores), and they provide valuable **Refined Mineral** drops [12].

### **Mining Level Unlocks**
While the sources provide detailed landmarks for early levels and the current level cap, they do not explicitly list unique world-area or item unlocks for every interval of ten (20, 30, 40, 50). The following specific unlocks are documented:

*   **Mining Level 5:** Unlocks the **Deep Caverns**, where you gain access to the **Lift Operator** for fast travel between mineral-specific floors [13-15].
*   **Mining Level 12:** Unlocks access to the **Dwarven Mines**, which introduces the **Heart of the Mountain (HotM)** skill tree and commissions [16, 17].
*   **Mining Level 20, 40, 60:** Each 20 levels contributes to a **SkyBlock Level milestone**, granting **+5 Magic Find** under the 2026 rework [4, 18].
*   **Mining Level 60:** This was established as the skill cap in update 0.11 and unlocks **Tier 12 Mining Minions** [19].

For higher-tier content beyond Mining 12, progression is primarily gated by your **Heart of the Mountain (HotM)** level rather than the Mining skill itself. For example, **HotM 4** is required to unlock the **Crystal Hollows** [20], and **HotM 10** is needed to forge the **Willow Wisp** mining deployable [21, 22].

---

## Q71: Explain the 5 dungeon classes: Berserker, Archer, Mage, Tank, Healer. What does each do and when is each used?

**Answer:**

In the Hypixel SkyBlock dungeon meta of 2026, the five classes—**Berserker, Archer, Mage, Tank, and Healer**—have evolved toward "Hybrid" roles, with heavy reliance on Wither Blade variants and Master Mode-specific gear setups [1, 2].

### **1. Berserker**
*   **What it does:** The Berserker is a **melee-focused damage class** that excels in close-quarters combat [2]. In 2026, the administration adjusted the game so that Berserker is now the **default dungeons class** [3].
*   **Primary Gear:** The standard meta involves using **Necron armor** with the **Ancient** reforge for both clearing and boss fights, often swapping to a **Diamond Head** for the boss phase [2].
*   **Meta Weapons:** The primary weapons used are the **Bouquet of Lies** or the **Valkyrie** [2].

### **2. Archer**
*   **What it does:** The Archer is the **primary long-range damage dealer**, utilizing shortbows to clear rooms and damage bosses from a distance [2]. 
*   **Primary Gear:** Archers typically use a mix of **Maxor and Necron armor** (Ancient) for clearing and full Necron with a Diamond Head for bosses [2]. A high-end strategy for Master Mode Floor 7 (M7) involves using a **Skeleton Master Chestplate** to grant "infinite arrows," which speeds up high-velocity runs [2].
*   **Meta Weapons:** The absolute meta weapon is the **Terminator**, typically enchanted with **Duplex** or **Soul Eater** [2].

### **3. Mage**
*   **What it does:** The Mage uses magic power and high intelligence to deal massive **Area of Effect (AoE) damage** through "Right-Click Mage" (RCM) abilities [1]. They are notably fragile ("squishy") until they acquire high-end healing weapons [4].
*   **Primary Gear:** Mages use **Storm armor** (Withered) paired with **Maxor boots** for clearing speed, and **Wither Goggles** for maximum ability damage during boss phases [2].
*   **Meta Weapons:** The **Hyperion** (with Ultimate Wise V) remains the apex tool for this class [1, 2].

### **4. Tank**
*   **What it does:** The Tank’s role has shifted into the **"Hybrid Tank"** [1]. Instead of purely absorbing damage, they act as damage dealers during the clearing phase and only switch to defensive duties during high-pressure boss moments [1].
*   **Primary Gear:** During the clearing phase, they wear high-damage gear like **3/4 Necron with Maxor boots** [1, 2]. For the boss, they switch to **"EHP" (Effective Health Pool) gear**, specifically **Goldor armor** and a **Reaper Mask**, usually paired with a **Blue Whale pet** [1, 2].
*   **Meta Weapons:** They use a **Terminator** for clearing and the **Astraea** (a defensive Wither Blade variant) for the boss phase [1, 2].

### **5. Healer**
*   **What it does:** The Healer provides **sustain and survivability** for the team. While once a niche role, they now utilize Wither Blade variants like the **Scylla** or **Astraea** for their specific defensive and healing properties [1].
*   **Primary Gear:** They use a mix of **Necron or Storm armor** for clearing and switch to **Goldor armor with a Mender Crown** for maximum healing output during bosses [2].
*   **Meta Weapons:** They typically use either a **Wither Blade** variant or a **Shortbow** [2].

### **When is each used?**
*   **Exploration/Clearing Phase:** All classes, including Tanks and Healers, prioritize **speed and AoE damage** (Cleaving gear) to finish rooms quickly [1, 2]. Mages use the Hyperion, while Archers and Hybrid Tanks use the Terminator [1, 2].
*   **Boss Phase:** Players switch to **Boss Gear** [2]. Tanks and Healers become essential in **Master Mode (M1–M7)** to manage the high damage output of bosses like Necron and Maxor, while Archers and Berserkers provide the necessary DPS to end the fight [1, 2].
*   **Master Mode Shift:** In the 2026 meta, the distinction between roles is most vital in Master Mode, where specific defensive pieces (like the Mender Crown or Goldor armor) are required to survive boss mechanics that would otherwise one-shot damage classes [1, 2].

---

## Q72: What is the dungeon class meta? Which class is best for beginners, which for mid-game, and which for endgame?

**Answer:**

In the first quarter of 2026, the dungeon class meta has matured with a heavy emphasis on **Hybrid class roles** and the dominance of **Wither Blade variants** like the Hyperion, Scylla, and Astraea [1]. The traditional separation of roles has shifted toward high-damage clearing setups that only transition to defensive gear during high-pressure boss phases [1].

### **Best Class for Beginners**
**Berserker** is widely considered the best class for beginners in 2026 [2]. 
*   **Ease of Progression:** The administration recently adjusted the default dungeon class from Archer to Berserk to better align with the natural progression for new players [3, 4]. 
*   **Survivability:** While classes like Mage are notoriously "squishy" and difficult to play effectively without high-end items, Berserkers can use sets like Shadow Assassin or even basic Adaptive armor to "breeze through" early floors [2, 5, 6].
*   **Accessibility:** Early weapons like a dungeonized Aspect of the Dragons (AOTD) with a Dragonfuse Glove are effective for clearing up to Floor 4 and 5 [5, 7].

### **Best Class for Mid-Game**
As players transition into the mid-game, **Berserker** remains the most reliable option, while **Mage** begins to emerge for those who can afford specialized gear [2, 6].
*   **The Mage Barrier:** Mid-game Mages often struggle with survivability until they obtain a Hyperion [2]. Those attempting mid-game Mage typically use **Wither Goggles** paired with **Wise Dragon** or **Storm armor** and a **Spirit Sceptre** or Glacial Scythe [6].
*   **Berserker Dominance:** Most mid-game players are encouraged to stay Berserker for more consistent runs unless they have the budget for a high-level Mage setup [2].
*   **Tank and Healer:** These roles are often filled in the mid-game to help lower-level parties survive harder floors like Floor 6 and 7, though they lack the high-speed efficiency of endgame hybrid variants [1, 8].

### **Endgame and Master Mode Meta**
The endgame meta (specifically for Master Mode M1–M7) is defined by **Hybrid roles** where every class contributes significantly to clear speed [1, 8].

| Class | Cleaving (Clear) Gear | Boss Phase Gear | Primary Meta Weapon |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Archer** | Maxor/Necron (Ancient) | Necron/Diamond Head | **Terminator** (Duplex/Soul Eater) [8] |
| **Mage** | Storm/Maxor (Withered) | Storm/Wither Goggles | **Hyperion** (Ult Wise V) [8] |
| **Berserker** | Necron (Ancient) | Necron/Diamond Head | **Valkyrie** or Bouquet of Lies [8] |
| **Tank** | Necron (Ancient) | Goldor/Reaper Mask | **Terminator** or Astraea [1, 8] |
| **Healer** | Necron/Storm | Goldor/Mender Crown | **Wither Blade** or Shortbow [8] |

**Key Endgame Strategic Shifts (2026):**
*   **The Hybrid Tank:** Endgame Tanks no longer sit in heavy armor for the entire run [1]. They use high-damage clearing sets (3/4 Necron with Maxor boots and a Terminator) and only switch to "EHP" gear like Goldor and a Blue Whale pet during intense boss moments [1, 9].
*   **Infinite Arrows:** A notable endgame Archer meta involves the **50/50 Skeleton Master Chestplate** from Master Mode 7, which provides "infinite arrows" to remove friction during high-speed runs [8].
*   **The Mage/Crow Synergy:** Endgame Mages have seen a shift in pet meta with the introduction of the **Crow pet** [10]. While the Sheep pet remains better for raw mana in dungeons, the Crow pet provides a "built-in refrigerate" effect, granting up to **+500 defense** after casting abilities, which is vital for survivability in Master Mode [11-13].

---

## Q73: When should players switch from Berserker to Archer or Mage? What triggers the transition and what gear is needed?

**Answer:**

In the 2026 Hypixel SkyBlock meta, players typically start as a **Berserker** because it is the sturdier "default" dungeons class, allowing players to "breeze through the game" while acquiring the levels and wealth needed for specialized roles [1, 2]. The transition to Archer or Mage is primarily triggered by the acquisition of high-end specialized weapons and reaching higher Catacombs levels for Master Mode [3, 4].

### **Transitioning to Mage**
The transition from Berserker to Mage is heavily gated by survivability and specific high-end gear.

*   **The Trigger:** Acquisition of a **Hyperion** (or other Wither Blade variants) is the definitive trigger for switching to an endgame Right-Click Mage (RCM) [2, 3]. Without a Hyperion, Mages are considered "very squishy" and difficult to play in higher floors [2].
*   **Endgame Gear Needed:**
    *   **Armor:** Storm Armor (Withered reforge) for boss phases, often mixed with Maxor boots for speed during clearing [4].
    *   **Helmet:** Wither Goggles [4].
    *   **Weapon:** Hyperion (Ultimate Wise V) [4]. Other variants like **Astraea** or **Scylla** are used for niche defense and healing [3].
    *   **Pets:** A Level 100 **Sheep** pet is the standard for dungeons due to its 25% mana increase [5]. The **Crow** pet is an alternative for players needing built-in defense and ability damage [6, 7].
*   **Mid-Game Alternative:** Before the Hyperion, players often use 3/4 Very Wise Dragon armor with Wither Goggles and a **Spirit Sceptre** or Glacial Scythe, though survivability remains a major hurdle [8].

### **Transitioning to Archer**
Archer is the primary damage-dealing class for high-tier dungeon boss phases, but it requires high-level equipment to be effective.

*   **The Trigger:** Obtaining a **Terminator** and reaching a high enough Catacombs level to run Master Mode (M1–M7) effectively triggers this shift [4, 9].
*   **Endgame Gear Needed:**
    *   **Armor:** Necron Armor (Ancient reforge) paired with a Diamond Boss Head for damage [4]. 
    *   **Clearing Set:** Archer clears are typically done using 3/4 Necron with **Maxor boots** for maximum movement speed [3, 4].
    *   **Chestplate Alternative:** The 50/50 version of the **Skeleton Master Chestplate** (from M7) is utilized by endgame archers to grant "infinite arrows," removing a significant point of friction in fast runs [4].
    *   **Weapon:** Terminator (equipped with Soul Eater or Duplex) [4].
    *   **Pets:** A Level 200 **Golden Dragon** is preferred for high-wealth players, while a Legendary **Skeleton** pet is a reliable budget alternative [8].

### **When to Stay Berserker**
Players should remain Berserker while they are still in the early-to-mid-game dungeon progression or if they lack a high-tier healing/damage weapon [2].
*   **Meta Weaponry:** Meta Berserkers in 2026 utilize the **Bouquet of Lies** or **Valkyrie** [4].
*   **Role Stability:** Berserker provides the necessary Effective Health (EHP) to survive lower Catacombs levels without the extreme coin investment required for a Hyperion or Terminator setup [2, 4].

---

## Q74: Explain the Berserker class in detail. What is the playstyle, best gear, best pets, and how does it perform at F5 vs F7 vs M7?

**Answer:**

The **Berserker** (or Berserk) class in Hypixel SkyBlock is a combat-focused dungeon class that prioritizes **high melee damage** and has recently evolved into a **hybrid role** within the late-game meta [1, 2]. In 2026, it became the **default dungeon class**, taking over from the Archer [3, 4].

### **Playstyle**
The Berserker playstyle revolves around high-speed clearing and massive single-target or small-area damage.
*   **Clearing vs. Boss Phases:** Modern Berserkers utilize a **clearing set** for mobility and area damage during the exploration phase of a dungeon, and then switch to a **boss set** for maximum survivability and DPS against the floor boss [2].
*   **Technical Changes:** The ability to activate the **Throwing Axe** by sneaking was recently **removed** to streamline gameplay [3, 4].
*   **Hybridization:** In high-level Master Mode, Berserkers often function in "hybrid" roles, occasionally utilizing multi-target tools like the **Terminator** to maintain efficiency during the clear before returning to melee weapons for the boss [1].

### **Best Gear**
The gear progression for Berserkers is heavily defined by Wither Armor variants and high-tier swords.
*   **Armor:**
    *   **Clearing:** Full **Necron armor** with the **Ancient reforge** is the standard [2].
    *   **Boss:** Necron armor paired with a **Diamond Head** (specifically the one matching the current floor) is considered best-in-slot [2]. Diamond Heads now carry over upgrades from Golden Heads [3].
    *   **Early/Mid-game:** Options include **Shadow Assassin (SA)** armor or **Adaptive armor** [5, 6]. 
*   **Weapons:**
    *   **Meta Primary:** The **Bouquet of Lies** and **Valkyrie** are the top choices for raw melee damage [2, 7].
    *   **Early/Mid-game:** The **Livid Dagger** and **Shadow Fury** remain reliable, while a **dungeonized Aspect of the Dragons (AOTD)** paired with a **Dragonfuse Glove** is a strong entry-level option [5, 6].

### **Best Pets**
The pet meta has shifted due to recent balance changes and the **Magic Find Revolution** [8].
*   **Golden Dragon (Gdrag):** Remains the king of damage, hitting for **2.4–2.6 million** in tests [9]. However, its Magic Find was recently nerfed to a passive +10, and its perks now grant **Defense** instead [10, 11].
*   **Ender Dragon:** A highly competitive alternative, dealing roughly **2–2.3 million** damage [12].
*   **Lion:** A solid budget option that competes closely with the Ender Dragon in terms of damage output [12].
*   **Ankylosaurus:** This pet was recently nerfed; its "Armored Tank" perk now grants **50% of your Defense as Strength** (capped at 500) [13, 14]. While strong for survivability outside dungeons, its cap is considered too low for high-tier dungeon usage [15, 16].
*   **Avoid - Baby Yeti:** Once a staple for Berserkers, the Baby Yeti is now **"absolutely cooked" in dungeons** because the strength-to-defense perk was removed [14, 17]. It is now primarily a Jerry Island fishing pet [17, 18].

### **Performance: F5 vs. F7 vs. M7**
The class's role and requirements change significantly as you progress through the floors.

| Floor | Performance & Strategy |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Floor 5 (F5)** | Highly efficient for early-mid game. Berserkers can easily "breeze through" this floor with a **Livid Dagger** or **AOTD** [19, 20]. Gear like SA armor is usually sufficient [6]. |
| **Floor 7 (F7)** | The standard endgame floor. Berserkers are required to have **3/4 Necron** and at least a **Golden/Diamond Head** [2, 21]. It is the stage where players typically transition to using **Wither Blade variants** [6]. |
| **Master Mode 7 (M7)** | The pinnacle of dungeon content. Performance here relies on **hyper-optimization**. Berserkers must act as a high-damage clearing force, often using **M7-specific Skeleton Master Chestplates** for niche perks like infinite arrows [2]. M7 runs are a major profit center, yielding approximately **50M coins/hour** [22]. |

---

## Q75: Explain the Archer class in detail. What is the playstyle, best gear, best pets, and when does it outperform Berserker?

**Answer:**

The **Archer class** in Hypixel SkyBlock is a specialized combat role that focuses on delivering high-damage ranged attacks, making it a critical component of high-level Dungeon teams [1, 2].

### **Archer Playstyle**
The Archer’s primary responsibility is **multi-target clearing** and dealing massive damage to bosses from a distance [2]. This playstyle relies heavily on the use of **shortbows**, which allow for rapid-fire attacks without the draw time of traditional bows [2]. In endgame scenarios, such as Master Mode (M1–M7), Archers are essential for their ability to eliminate large groups of mobs quickly and safely engage high-pressure boss phases [1, 2].

### **Best Gear for Archer**
The optimal gear for an Archer is divided between "clearing" and "boss" setups:
*   **Armor:** For clearing rooms, the meta involves **Maxor or Necron armor** with the **Ancient** reforge [2]. For boss phases, players switch to **Necron armor** paired with a **Diamond Head** (such as the Diamond Necron Head) to maximize damage output [2, 3].
*   **Chestplate Alternative:** A significant specialized option for high-speed runs is the **Skeleton Master Chestplate** (specifically the 50/50 version from M7), which provides the unique perk of **infinite arrows**, removing a major point of mechanical friction [2].
*   **Weapon:** The definitive primary weapon is the **Terminator**, typically equipped with either **Duplex** (for boss damage) or **Soul Eater** (for clearing) [2].
*   **Equipment:** The combat meta in 2026 emphasizes **David’s Cloak** for general utility, having surpassed the Shadow Assassin Cloak in effectiveness [4].

### **Best Pets**
*   **Top Tier:** The **Golden Dragon** is the premier damage pet, though its Magic Find stats were reduced in favor of **Defense** perks in early 2026 [5, 6]. It remains the benchmark for raw combat damage [7].
*   **Budget Option:** The **Legendary Skeleton pet** is recommended as a "cheap and decent" alternative specifically tailored for Archers [8].
*   **Defensive Hybrid:** The **Ankylosaurus pet** can be a solid choice outside of Dungeons if an Archer needs to sacrifice some offense for **Defense and Strength** scaling, though it is considered "cooked" for Dungeon use due to stat caps [7, 9, 10].

### **Archer vs. Berserker**
Archers typically outperform Berserkers in the following scenarios:
*   **Multi-Target Efficiency:** Using a Terminator allows Archers to hit multiple targets simultaneously, which is generally more efficient for clearing large Dungeon rooms than the close-quarters combat of a Berserker [2, 11].
*   **Master Mode Scaling:** In Master Mode, the Archer’s ability to deal damage from a distance is often safer and more reliable than the Berserker's melee-focused approach [1, 2].
*   **Boss Phase DPS:** During boss fights like Nekron or Storm (F7/M7), the Archer’s rapid damage ticks are superior for shredding high-health targets [2, 12].

However, it is notable that as of update 0.24.2, the **default Dungeon class** was adjusted from Archer to **Berserk**, reflecting a shift in how the game introduces players to Dungeoneering [13, 14]. While Berserkers excel at close-range survivability and single-target bursts with weapons like the **Valkyrie**, the Archer remains the apex for raw, consistent ranged damage in the 2026 meta [2].

---

## Q76: Explain the Mage class in detail. What is the playstyle, what spells are used, what gear is optimal, and when does it shine?

**Answer:**

The **Mage** is a high-damage, ability-focused class in Hypixel SkyBlock that primarily utilizes **mana** (intelligence) to fuel powerful magical weapons [1, 2]. The class is defined by its ability to deliver massive **Area of Effect (AoE)** damage, making it the premier choice for clearing large groups of enemies quickly [3-5].

### **Playstyle**
The Mage playstyle generally revolves around **Right-Click Mage (RCM)** mechanics, where players spam weapon abilities to destroy mobs from a distance [6-8]. While Mages possess immense offensive power, they are notoriously **"squishy"** (having low effective health) until they reach the endgame, often requiring specialized gear or enchantments to survive high-pressure situations [2, 9]. Efficiency is key to the Mage playstyle; higher-level Mages use their AoE capabilities to kill dozens of mobs in the time it takes a lower-level player to kill one [5].

### **Spells and Weapons**
Mages rely on specific magic weapons that offer unique spells and abilities:
*   **Hyperion:** This Wither Blade variant is the **apex tool for RCM players**, offering immense damage and utility [6, 7, 10].
*   **Spirit Scepter:** A staple mid-game weapon known for its consistent AoE damage and lack of ability cooldown [7, 8, 11].
*   **Fire Veil Wand:** Frequently used alongside primary weapons to provide passive AoE damage around the player [4, 12, 13].
*   **Glacial Scythe and Yeti Sword:** These serve as alternative high-damage mage weapons for various stages of the game [8, 14].
*   **Bonzo Staff:** Often used by early-game Mages or for specific mobility and clearing tasks [11, 15].

### **Optimal Gear**
To maximize damage and mana pools, Mages require specialized equipment:
*   **Armor:** **Storm’s Armor** is the primary choice for damage scaling, while **Aurora Armor** is also highly effective [7, 10, 14]. **Maxor’s Boots** are sometimes substituted to increase movement speed during dungeon clears [10].
*   **Helmet:** **Wither Goggles** (and their lower-tier variants like Goggles or Shadow Goggles) are mandatory for their massive multiplier to ability damage [7, 10, 14].
*   **Enchantments:** **Ultimate Wise V** is critical on weapons to reduce the mana cost of spamming abilities [10, 11]. For armor, the **Refrigerate** enchantment is essential, as it grants defense based on mana spent, significantly improving survivability [16-18].
*   **Pets:** The **Sheep Pet** is the standard for its high intelligence and mana cost reduction [11, 19, 20]. The **Crow Pet** is a viable alternative that offers a built-in defense buff when casting abilities [9, 19]. Endgame players may use a **Golden Dragon** for maximum damage, though it requires a significant bank balance to be effective [21, 22].
*   **Attributes:** **Shellwise** is highly recommended to grant knockback immunity, which is vital when surrounded by mobs [12, 23].

### **When the Mage Class Shines**
The Mage class is most dominant in scenarios requiring high-speed mob extermination:
*   **Dungeon Clearing:** Mages are essential for fast room clears in both standard and Master Mode dungeons due to their AoE range [3, 24, 25].
*   **Year of the Witch:** This event demands a high **"kill-per-hour" strategy** to collect various mob-type ingredients, a task perfectly suited for the Hyperion's broad damage radius [3, 26].
*   **Kuudra:** Mages excel at clearing the numerous mobs that spawn during the fight, allowing teammates to focus on boss mechanics [7, 27].
*   **General Grinding:** Whether farming ghosts in the Dwarven Mines or clearing the Hub Graveyard, the Mage's ability to "set and forget" high damage output makes them highly efficient for RNG-heavy drops [5, 28].

---

## Q77: Explain the Tank class. Is it relevant in the current meta? When would players use Tank?

**Answer:**

The **Tank** class in Hypixel SkyBlock is a specialized role focused on high **Effective Health (EHP)** and damage mitigation to protect teammates during difficult encounters [1]. In the current 2026 meta, the class has matured into the **"Hybrid Tank"** role, which balances high damage for clearing rooms with heavy defensive gear for boss fights [1].

### **Class Gear and Mechanics**
The modern Tank meta relies on shifting between two distinct gear setups depending on the phase of the dungeon:
*   **Clearing Gear:** During the exploration and clearing phase of a dungeon, Tanks typically wear **Ancient Necron armor** with Maxor boots and use a **Terminator** to maintain high speed and damage [1, 2]. 
*   **Boss Gear:** During high-pressure boss phases, Tanks switch to **Goldor armor** paired with a **Reaper Mask** for maximum survivability [1, 2].
*   **Primary Weapons:** The **Terminator** remains the meta choice for damage, while the **Astraea** (a Wither Blade variant) is utilized for its defensive and healing properties [1, 2].
*   **Pets:** Following recent nerfs to the Baby Yeti pet, which removed its strength-to-defense conversion perk, the **Blue Whale** has become the standard replacement for dungeon survivability [3]. The **Turtle pet** is also noted as a viable alternative for some players [3].

### **Relevance in the Meta**
The Tank class is **highly relevant** and considered essential for high-level **Master Mode (M1–M7)** runs [1]. The complexity of these floors requires a player who can soak up damage during boss phases that would otherwise instantly defeat other classes [1, 4]. However, the traditional "pure Tank" has been largely replaced by the Hybrid model to ensure the team can clear rooms efficiently without sacrificing speed [1]. 

### **When to Use Tank**
Players typically use the Tank class in the following scenarios:
*   **Master Mode Dungeons:** It is the standard for Floors M1 through M7, particularly during the boss fights where EHP is critical [1].
*   **High-Pressure Combat:** Tanks are utilized when a team needs a dedicated player to handle the mechanics of a boss, such as in **Floor 7 or M7**, while others focus on DPS [2].
*   **Solo Survival (Non-Dungeon):** Outside of dungeons, players might use Tank-oriented pets like the **Ankylosaurus** to survive difficult Slayer bosses (such as Enderman) without completely losing their offensive capabilities [5, 6]. The Ankylosaurus allows players to gain strength based on their defense, providing a bridge between survivability and damage [7, 8].
*   **Countering Recent Nerfs:** Because the Baby Yeti has been shifted into a specialized Jerry Island fishing pet and "Necron tanking" has faced recent adjustments, players must now lean more heavily into dedicated defensive gear like Goldor and Blue Whale pets to maintain their effectiveness [3, 9, 10].

---

## Q78: Explain the Healer class. Is it used in the current meta? What does it provide to a party?

**Answer:**

The **Healer** class is one of the five primary roles in the Hypixel SkyBlock dungeon system. In the current Q1 2026 meta, it has adapted to the "Hybrid" class paradigm dominant in Master Mode (M1–M7) [1, 2].

### **Meta Status**
The Healer class remains a recognized part of the **Master Mode dungeon meta**. According to current analytical assessments, the 2026 meta emphasizes **Hybrid roles**, where players shift between high-damage and specialized utility gear depending on the phase of the dungeon run [1, 2].

### **What the Healer Provides to a Party**
The Healer's primary contribution is ensuring **survivability** through healing and defensive support, especially during high-pressure boss fights [1].
*   **Healing Utility:** Healers utilize specialized equipment like the **Mender Crown** to amplify healing effectiveness [2]. They often use **Wither Blade variants** such as the **Scylla** or **Astraea**, which are favored in the modern meta for their niche healing and defensive properties [1].
*   **Hybrid Combat Support:** During the clearing (exploration) phase, modern Healers are expected to contribute to damage. They typically wear **Necron or Storm armor** for speed and power, using weapons like a Wither Blade or a **Shortbow** [2].
*   **Boss Phase Tankiness:** When transitioning to boss phases, the Healer shifts into a high-survivability role, switching to **Goldor armor** and the Mender Crown to sustain themselves and the party through intense damage [2].

### **Current Meta Gear (Q1 2026)**
| Phase | Gear Used |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Clearing Gear** | Necron or Storm armor [2] |
| **Boss Gear** | Goldor armor with a **Mender Crown** [2] |
| **Primary Meta Weapon** | **Wither Blade** (Astraea/Scylla) or Shortbow [1, 2] |

While other classes like the "Hybrid Tank" have evolved to handle significant damage and protection, the Healer provides the essential specialized healing needed for the most difficult content in the game [1].

## Q79: How do class levels work in dungeons? How is class XP earned, and what level should each class be before attempting each floor?

**Answer:**

In Hypixel SkyBlock, the dungeon system revolves around the **Catacombs**, where players choose one of five specialized classes: **Archer, Mage, Berserker, Tank, or Healer** [1, 2]. Progression is defined by your Catacombs (Cata) level and your individual class levels.

### How Class Levels Work
Class levels provide passive stat boosts that scale while you are inside a dungeon. As your level increases, your class-specific abilities become more potent. For example, the **Mage** class utilizes mana-based abilities, while the **Tank** focuses on effective health (EHP) and protecting teammates [3-5].

*   **Gear Requirements:** Many high-tier dungeon items are "hard-gated" by your Catacombs level. For instance, **Storm’s armor** requires **Cata level 24** and a successful Floor 7 completion to be used [5].
*   **Class Shifts:** The 2026 meta emphasizes "Hybrid" roles, such as the **Hybrid Tank**, who uses damage gear (like Necron armor and a Terminator) for clearing rooms and switches to defensive gear (Goldor armor and a Blue Whale pet) for boss phases [3].

### How Class XP is Earned
Class XP is primarily earned by completing dungeon floors. The amount of XP you receive is influenced by several factors:
*   **Floor Difficulty:** Higher floors and **Master Mode** (M1–M7) provide significantly more XP than early floors [3, 6].
*   **Dungeon Score:** Your final score (up to 300 or more) determines the quality of your rewards and the amount of XP granted [7, 8].
*   **Secrets and Exploration:** Finding secrets in rooms is a critical part of the scoring system. Maps will display a white checkmark instead of green if a room has undiscovered secrets [9].
*   **Class Selection:** You must have a class selected (e.g., Mage) when entering the floor to earn XP for that specific class [7].

### Recommended Levels Before Attempting Each Floor
While the game has strict minimum entry requirements (such as **Combat level 15** to enter Dungeons), community standards and gear requirements dictate when you should move up [7]. 

Based on the sources, here are the general level recommendations for progression:

| Floor | Suggested Level/Requirement | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Entrance** | Combat Level 15 [7] | The starting point for all dungeon progression. |
| **Floor 1** | Early Cata Levels [7] | Often run with carries for beginners to unlock the Emerald Chest [7]. |
| **Floor 4-5** | **Cata 12+** (Minimum) | Players at Cata 12 report struggling and needing carries for Floor 4 [10]. |
| **Floor 7** | **Cata 24** [5] | Required for top-tier gear like Storm's armor and Wither Goggles [5]. |
| **Master Mode** | **High Cata (30+)** [3] | Requires specialized sets and "Hybrid" strategies to survive boss pressures [3]. |

**Strategy Note:** It is highly recommended that players be able to fill multiple roles (e.g., both DPS and Stunner in high-tier content like Kuudra) to remain flexible in party finders [11]. For those struggling with survivability, the **Berserker** class is often recommended for "breezing through" until you can afford high-end Mage gear like the Hyperion [12].

## Q80: What is the best way to level dungeon classes quickly? Which floor gives the most class XP per hour?

**Answer:**

Based on the sources provided, there is no direct chart ranking floors by numerical Class XP per hour; however, the documentation of the **2026 combat and dungeon meta** identifies the most efficient floors and strategies for high-speed progression.

### **The Best Floors for Progression**
*   **Master Mode Floor 7 (M7):** This floor is highlighted as the pinnacle of the current meta for experienced players, generating the highest estimated profit (approx. **50M coins/hour**) and utilizing specialized gear designed for "high-speed dungeon runs" [1, 2].
*   **Other Master Mode Floors:** Floors such as **M4 (40M/hr)**, **M5**, and **M6 (30M/hr)** are also cited as standard endgame activities, though M7 remains superior for those who can complete it consistently [2, 3].
*   **Early Game:** For brand-new players or those pursuing specific goals (like Bingo), the recommended strategy is getting **carried through Floor 1 (F1)** to achieve high scores (e.g., a 230 score) and unlock rewards quickly [4].

### **Strategies for Leveling Quickly**
To maximize efficiency and speed in dungeon runs, the 2026 meta emphasizes the following optimizations:

*   **Adopt "Hybrid" Class Roles:** The meta has shifted toward hybrid roles to handle both clearing and boss phases efficiently. For example, the **Hybrid Tank** uses high-damage sets (like 3/4 Necron with Maxor boots and a Terminator) for clearing and only switches to EHP gear (Goldor and Blue Whale) for intense boss phases [5].
*   **Use High-Speed Archer Gear:** Archers often utilize the **Skeleton Master Chestplate** (specifically the 50/50 version from M7). While it has lower health than Necron armor, its perk providing **infinite arrows** removes a major point of friction, allowing for much faster completion times in high-speed runs [1].
*   **Optimized Class Gear:**
    *   **Mage:** Utilize **Storm/Maxor** gear with a **Hyperion** (Ultimate Wise V) and **Wither Goggles** for boss phases [1].
    *   **Berserker:** Use **Necron** (Ancient) with a **Valkyrie** or **Bouquet of Lies**. Notably, the default dungeon class has been adjusted from Archer to **Berserk** in recent updates [1, 6].
*   **Movement Items:** For clearing rooms at maximum speed, mobility items like the **Aspect of the End (AOTE)** and the **Grappling Hook** are considered essential to minimize time between mob encounters [7, 8].

While not explicitly from the sources, higher floor tiers and faster completion times generally correlate with higher Class XP yields in Hypixel SkyBlock. The focus on **M7** and **high-speed clearing sets** suggests these are the current standards for maximizing your hourly gains [1, 2].

## Q81: Give a complete guide for each dungeon floor F1-F7: boss name, entry requirement, key drops, and recommended class level.

**Answer:**

Based on the provided sources, here is a guide for the Dungeon Floors F1 through F7 in the Catacombs.

### **Floor 1 (F1)**
*   **Boss Name:** **Bonzo** [1, 2].
*   **Entry Requirement:** **Combat Level 15** and completion of the Entrance floor [1].
*   **Key Drops:** **Bonzo's Staff**, **Bonzo's Mask**, and Enchanted Books like **Feather Falling VI** or **Infinite Quiver VI** [3, 4].
*   **Recommended Class Level:** Players typically use this floor to start their Dungeoneering journey after reaching the Combat 15 requirement [1].

### **Floor 2 (F2)**
*   **Boss Name:** **Scarf** [2].
*   **Entry Requirement:** Completion of Floor 1.
*   **Key Drops:** Redstone Key (implied by zone mechanics) and various dungeon equipment [5]. 
*   **Recommended Class Level:** Low Catacombs levels (1–5).

### **Floor 3 (F3)**
*   **Boss Name:** **The Professor** [2].
*   **Entry Requirement:** Completion of Floor 2.
*   **Key Drops:** **Adaptive Armor** and **Adaptive Blade**, which are designed to change stats based on your selected dungeon class [5, 6].
*   **Recommended Class Level:** Catacombs Level 5–10.

### **Floor 4 (F4)**
*   **Boss Name:** **Thorn** [2, 7].
*   **Entry Requirement:** Completion of Floor 3.
*   **Key Drops:** **Spirit Wing**, **Spirit Bone**, and the **Spirit Sceptre** (a primary weapon for Mages) [8, 9]. *Note: The Spirit Mask has been moved to the Master Mode Floor 4 drop table and is no longer in the standard F4 or Dark Auction loot pools [10, 11].*
*   **Recommended Class Level:** **Catacombs Level 12–15**; players at this level often use **Adaptive Armor** to clear the floor [12].

### **Floor 5 (F5)**
*   **Boss Name:** **Livid** [2].
*   **Entry Requirement:** Completion of Floor 4.
*   **Key Drops:** **Shadow Assassin Armor**, **Livid Dagger**, and the **Shadow Fury** [13, 14].
*   **Recommended Class Level:** **Catacombs Level 15–20** [8, 12].

### **Floor 6 (F6)**
*   **Boss Name:** **Sadan** [2].
*   **Entry Requirement:** Completion of Floor 5.
*   **Key Drops:** **Necromancer Lord Armor** and the **Giant's Sword** [13].
*   **Recommended Class Level:** **Catacombs Level 20–24** [8].

### **Floor 7 (F7)**
*   **Boss Name:** **The Wither Lords** (**Maxor**, **Storm**, **Goldor**, and **Necron**) [2, 15].
*   **Entry Requirement:** **Catacombs Level 24** [8].
*   **Key Drops:** **Necron's Handle** (used to craft Wither Blades like **Hyperion**, **Valkyrie**, **Scylla**, or **Astraea**) and the four **Wither Armor** sets (Necron, Storm, Goldor, and Maxor) [15-17].
*   **Recommended Class Level:** **Catacombs Level 24–30+**; at this stage, Mages typically aim for **Storm's Armor** and a **Hyperion**, while Archers use **Necron's Armor** and a **Terminator** [8, 15].

### **General Class Recommendations for Higher Floors (F5–F7)**
The 2026 meta emphasizes specific gear setups to survive and clear efficiently:
*   **Archer:** 3/4 Necron with Maxor Boots and a **Terminator** (Duplex or Soul Eater) [15].
*   **Mage:** 3/4 Storm with Wither Goggles and a **Hyperion** (Ultimate Wise V) [15].
*   **Berserker:** Necron Armor with a **Valkyrie** or **Bouquet of Lies** [15].
*   **Tank:** **Goldor's Armor** paired with a **Reaper Mask** and a Blue Whale pet for effective health (EHP) [15, 16, 18].
*   **Healer:** Goldor/Storm mix with a **Mender Crown** [15].

## Q82: What is the best floor to farm for coins at mid-game (Catacombs 15-25)? Compare F4, F5, F6.

**Answer:**

For mid-game players in the **Catacombs 15–25** range, the best floor to farm for coins depends on whether you prioritize consistent hourly profit or are willing to gamble for rare "RNG" drops. With the recent addition of the **Year of the Witch** event in early 2026, the profitability of these floors has shifted significantly due to high demand for specific mob-type ingredients [1, 2].

### **Floor Comparison (F4, F5, F6)**

| Floor | Primary Coin Sources | Year of the Witch Synergy | Final Verdict |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Floor 4 (F4)** | **Spirit Wings** and **Spirit Bones**. Consistent profit from chest rewards. | **Extremely High.** Best floor for **Animal** (Frog Legs) and **Arcane** (Spell Soot) drops [3-5]. | **Best for Consistency.** Currently the most profitable mid-game floor for hourly rates [6, 7]. |
| **Floor 5 (F5)** | **Shadow Assassin (SA)** armor pieces and **Livid Daggers** [8]. | **Low.** Passively grants some Undead/Skeletal drops, but not an optimal farming spot [9]. | **Best for Personal Gear.** Good for players who still need their own SA set, but profit is less stable than F4. |
| **Floor 6 (F6)** | **Necromancer Lord** armor and the **Giant's Sword** (high-value gamble) [10]. | **Medium.** Excellent for **Construct** (Console Panels) drops from boss mobs [3, 11]. | **Best for RNG Gamblers.** High potential profit if you drop a Giant's Sword, but much slower than F4/F5. |

### **Floor 4: The Consistency King**
Floor 4 is currently regarded as the superior floor for mid-game coin generation. Even in its Master Mode variant (M4), players report it generating roughly **40 million coins per hour**, which outperforms M5 and M6 (around 30m/hr) [6]. 
*   **Ingredient Farming:** During the Year of the Witch event, F4 is the prime location for **Frog Legs** and **Spell Soot**. Players report obtaining over a stack of Frog Legs in as little as five minutes [4, 12].
*   **Spirit Items:** Spirit Wings and Spirit Bones remain in high demand for crafting essential mid-to-late game gear. 

### **Floor 6: The "Giant" Gamble**
Floor 6 remains popular for players chasing the **Giant’s Sword**, which is one of the most valuable dungeon drops [10]. 
*   **Construct Ingredients:** It is also a viable spot for **Console Panels** (Construct type), particularly from the golems and terracotta mobs in the boss room [11, 13]. 
*   **Difficulty Note:** For players at Cata 15, F6 may be difficult to run efficiently without a strong party, making it less profitable per hour than a fast F4 or F5 clear.

### **Important Meta Shift (Q1 2026)**
*   **Spirit Mask:** As of update 0.24.3, the **Spirit Mask** has been moved exclusively to the Master Mode Floor 4 drop table [14, 15]. If you are looking for this item specifically, you can no longer obtain it from regular Floor 4 chests.
*   **Active vs. Passive Income:** While dungeons are fun, the current economy favors "active" methods. For comparison, a maxed **Pest Farming** setup can net **80M/hr**, and **Glacite Shafting** earns around **60M/hr** [16]. If your goal is raw coins rather than Dungeoneering XP, these methods may eventually outpace F4-F6 [16, 17].

**Final Verdict:** If you are within the Catacombs 15–25 range and want the most reliable income, **Floor 4** is the best choice, especially if you take advantage of the high demand for **Year of the Witch ingredients** [3, 6].

---

## Q83: What makes F5 (Livid) valuable? What drops make it worth farming, how much can players earn per run, and what is the minimum setup needed?

**Answer:**

In the Catacombs, **Floor 5 (F5)** is primarily valuable as a milestone for mid-game combat progression, specifically for the acquisition of **Shadow Assassin Armor** [1, 2]. 

### **Valuable Drops and Farming Incentives**
The primary reason players farm F5 is to obtain gear that serves as a staple for physical damage setups. Key drops include:
*   **Shadow Assassin Armor**: This set was introduced with Floor 5 and remains a core armor for players transitioning into higher-tier dungeons and general combat [1, 2].
*   **Livid Dagger**: A signature weapon from this floor, though it notably received a nerf shortly after its release [3]. 
*   **Bedrock Chest Rewards**: Completing the floor allows access to the Bedrock Chest, which contains the floor's rarest and most valuable loots [1].

### **Estimated Earnings**
The provided sources do not list a specific coin value per individual run for standard Floor 5. However, for context regarding dungeon profitability in the early 2026 meta:
*   **Master Mode 5 (M5)** is estimated to yield approximately **30 million coins per hour** [4].
*   Dungeons in general are noted for being enjoyable, but other activities like **Hunting** can yield much higher rates, exceeding **150 million coins per hour** [5, 6].

### **Minimum Setup Requirements**
A basic setup of **Adaptive Armor** and an **Aspect of the Dragons (AOTD)** is generally considered insufficient for comfortable F5 runs, as players at Catacombs Level 12 with this gear often require "carries" just to clear Floor 4 [7, 8].

For a more viable setup, the sources suggest:
*   **Class Recommendation**: Swapping to the **Berserker** class is often recommended for players who find Mage too "squishy" before obtaining endgame weapons like the Hyperion [9].
*   **Armor & Weapons**: Players around Catacombs Level 20 often utilize **3/4 Shadow Assassin Armor** paired with a **Livid Dagger** or **Shadow Fury** [2].
*   **Mage Alternative**: A common mid-progression Mage setup includes **3/4 Very Wise Dragon Armor** with **Wither Goggles** and a **Spirit Scepter** [2].
*   **Combat Stats**: It is essential to balance **Strength and Critical Damage** in a 1:1 ratio and reach **100% Critical Chance** to ensure consistent damage output [10-12]. Utilizing the **"Fortuitous" power** from Maxwell is a recommended starting point for achieving this [10, 13].

## Q84: What makes F7 (Necron) the endgame dungeon? What drops, how much per run, and what gear is needed for fast clears?

**Answer:**

Floor 7 (F7) and its Master Mode counterpart (M7) remain the definitive endgame dungeons in 2026 due to their complex mechanical boss phases, high skill requirements, and a loot table that contains the game's most powerful combat gear [1-3].

### What Makes F7/M7 the Endgame Dungeon?
Floor 7 represents a significant jump in complexity compared to previous floors, featuring the multi-phase Necron boss fight [4]. In the 2026 meta, it is defined by:
*   **The Master Mode Shift:** M7 is currently the highest-tier challenge, yielding the best experience and most valuable loot, such as the Diamond Necron Head [3, 5].
*   **Active Money-Making:** Unlike passive methods, M7 requires intense participation and coordination, making it one of the few activities that can compete with or exceed the "real-world parity" of generator methods [2, 6].
*   **Resource Grinding:** Floor 7 is the optimal location for gathering specific "Year of the Witch" ingredients like **Funny Bones** (from skeletal mobs in Maxor/Storm phases) and **Brittle Powder** (from withered mobs) [7-9].

### Key Drops and Profits
While exact per-run averages fluctuate with the Bazaar and Auction House, **M7 yields approximately 50 million coins per hour** for high-efficiency teams [2]. 

| Major Drops | Category | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Necron's Handle** | Weapon Core | Used to craft Wither Blades (Hyperion, Valkyrie, etc.) [10, 11]. |
| **Wither Armor** | Armor | Base for Necron, Storm, Goldor, and Maxor sets [11]. |
| **Wither Scrolls** | Power-ups | Implosion, Wither Shield, and Shadow Warp [10]. |
| **Diamond Necron Head** | Boss Collection | Recipe unlocked via the boss collection milestone [5]. |
| **Recombobulator 3000**| Upgrade | Common high-value chest drop [10, 12]. |
| **50/50 Skeleton Master**| Archer Gear | Dropped in M7; provides "infinite arrows" for high-speed runs [13]. |

### Recommended Gear for Fast Clears (2026 Meta)
The current meta emphasizes "Hybrid" class roles, where players use high-damage sets for clearing rooms and switch to specialized gear for boss phases [1].

*   **Archer:**
    *   **Clearing:** 3/4 Maxor or Necron (Ancient) with a **Skeleton Master Chestplate** (50/50 M7 version) for infinite arrows [13].
    *   **Boss:** Full Necron or a **Diamond Necron Head** [13].
    *   **Weapon:** **Terminator** (Duplex or Soul Eater) [10, 13].
*   **Mage:**
    *   **Clearing:** Storm/Maxor armor (Withered/Necrotic) [13].
    *   **Boss:** Storm armor with **Wither Goggles** [13].
    *   **Weapon:** **Hyperion** (Ultimate Wise V) is the absolute standard for Right-Click Mage (RCM) [1, 13].
*   **Tank (Hybrid Tank):**
    *   **Clearing:** 3/4 Necron with Maxor boots and a Terminator to assist in room clearing [1].
    *   **Boss:** Switch to **Goldor armor** and a **Reaper Mask** for maximum effective health [1, 13].
    *   **Pet:** **Blue Whale** (replaces the nerfed Baby Yeti for most dungeon players) or **Turtle** [14].
*   **Berserker:**
    *   **Gear:** Ancient Necron with a Diamond Head [13].
    *   **Weapon:** **Bouquet of Lies** or **Valkyrie** [13].

**Important Optimization Tips:**
*   **Attributes:** Max out the **Shellwise** attribute to gain immunity to all knockback, which is critical for maintaining DPS during boss phases [15, 16].
*   **Equipment:** **David's Cloak** has surpassed the Shadow Assassin Cloak for general combat utility in late-game setups [17].
*   **Passive Magic Find:** Following the 2026 Magic Find (MF) rework, you no longer need to gear-swap to MF armor like Sorrow at the end of a run; MF is now a permanent passive stat derived from SkyBlock levels and accessories [18, 19].

## Q85: Explain Master Mode dungeons. What are the entry requirements for MM1-MM7, and what makes MM7 the most profitable?

**Answer:**

**Master Mode** represents the endgame escalation of the Catacombs dungeons, requiring high-level optimization and "Hybrid" class roles [1, 2]. In 2026, the meta for Master Mode (M1–M7) emphasizes players using high-damage sets for clearing rooms (such as **Necron armor** with **Maxor boots** and a **Terminator**) and switching to high-survivability "EHP" gear (like **Goldor armor** and a **Blue Whale pet**) for boss phases [1].

### Entry Requirements
While the provided sources do not list a specific table of Catacombs level requirements for each Master Mode floor, they outline the following core requirements for dungeon progression and high-tier gear:

*   **General Dungeon Entry:** Requires **Combat Level 15** [3].
*   **Floor 7 Completion:** Required to use standard endgame mage gear like **Storm’s armor**, which also carries a **Catacombs Level 24** requirement [4].
*   **Master Mode 4 (M4):** A specific requirement for obtaining the **Spirit Mask**, which was moved to the M4 drop table in early 2026. It drops from the **Obsidian Chest** (costing 15M coins) and requires a high dungeon score to obtain [5, 6].
*   **Master Mode 7 (M7):** Introduced in March 2022, this floor represents the current peak of dungeon difficulty [7]. Access to the highest tier of rewards, such as the **Diamond Necron Head**, is gated behind M7 boss collections [6].

### Why Master Mode 7 (MM7) is the Most Profitable
MM7 is consistently cited as the most lucrative floor, yielding approximately **50 million coins per hour** [8]. Its profitability is driven by several factors:

*   **High-Value Meta Drops:** MM7 is the source of the **50/50 Skeleton Master Chestplate**. Although it has lower effective health than Necron armor, it is highly valued in the endgame archer meta because its perk provides "infinite arrows," removing a major friction point in high-speed runs [2].
*   **Exclusive Upgrades:** Players can obtain the recipe for the **Diamond Necron Head** through M7 boss collections. This update allows Diamond Heads to carry over upgrades from Golden Heads, making them essential for hyper-optimized combat setups [6].
*   **Year of the Witch Synergy:** During the "Year of the Witch" event, MM7 is the most efficient location to grind for rare skeletal and withered ingredients. Mobs in the **Maxor and Storm phases** drop **Funny Bones** (skeletal) and **Brittle Powder** (withered) in large quantities (roughly a stack per run), which are required for permanent Magic Find stews [9-13].
*   **Consistent Loot Chests:** High-tier chests in F7/M7 can contain **Recombobulator 3000s** and **Fuming Potato Books**, maintaining a high baseline of profit [14].
*   **Economic Benchmark:** MM7 is one of the few "active" money-making methods that can approach or exceed the "real-wage" yield of SkyBlock labor (calculated at ~62.3 million coins per hour based on the cost of Booster Cookies) [15, 16].

## Q86: What is S+ rank in dungeons and why does it matter? How do players consistently achieve S+ rank?

**Answer:**

In Hypixel SkyBlock Dungeons, **S+ rank** is the highest performance rating achievable in a run, determined by a player's final score [1, 2].

### Why S+ Rank Matters
Achieving a high rank is critical because it determines which reward chests are unlocked at the end of a floor.
*   **Chests and Loot**: Specific score thresholds are required to access high-tier chests. For instance, players need a **score of at least 230** to unlock the **Emerald Dungeon Chest** [1]. Higher ranks are necessary to access the **Bedrock Chest** (introduced on Floor 5) or the **Obsidian Chest** (on Master Mode Floor 4), which contain the rarest drops [3-5].
*   **Dungeon Meta**: The Dungeoneering category in the Museum specifically measures a player's progress within the dungeon meta, and achieving top ranks is a standard metric for progression [6].

### How Players Consistently Achieve S+ Rank
Achieving S+ requires a near-perfect score, which is composed of several factors including exploration, speed, and skill.

**1. Finding Secrets**
Finding "secrets" hidden throughout dungeon rooms is vital for a high score. The game provides quality-of-life UI features to help:
*   **Map Indicators**: The dungeon map uses check marks to indicate secret completion. A **white check mark** indicates a room has undiscovered secrets, while a **green check mark** means all secrets in that room have been found [7].
*   **Reduced Requirements**: Recent updates have reduced the total number of secrets necessary on some floors to reach the full exploration score [2].

**2. Optimized Clearing Gear**
Speed is essential to minimize time penalties. Players use high-damage Area of Effect (AoE) weapons and specific class setups to clear rooms instantly:
*   **Meta Weapons**: The **Hyperion** remains the apex tool for Mages (RCM), while Archers utilize the **Terminator** (with Duplex or Soul Eater) [8, 9]. Berserkers often use the **Bouquet of Lies** or **Valkyrie** [9].
*   **Hybrid Roles**: To stay efficient, the "Hybrid Tank" meta has emerged. These players use high-damage clearing sets (3/4 Necron with Maxor boots) to speed through the exploration phase, only switching to defensive gear (Goldor armor and Blue Whale pet) for high-pressure boss phases [8].

**3. Efficiency and Speed**
*   **High Speed Navigation**: To move between rooms faster, players permanentely equip mobility items like the **Rogue Sword** (for speed boosts), **Grappling Hook**, and **Aspect of the End (AOTE)** or **Aspect of the Void** for teleportation [10-12].
*   **Infinite Arrows**: High-level Archers utilize a "50/50" version of the **Skeleton Master Chestplate** from M7, which provides infinite arrows and removes the friction of ammunition management during high-speed runs [9].

**4. Avoiding Deaths**
Deaths significantly penalize the dungeon score. Players use specialized gear to survive:
*   **Survivability Gear**: Items like the **Spirit Mask** (moved to the Master Mode Floor 4 drop table) provide a second chance upon death [4, 5, 13]. 
*   **Class Specific Defense**: Healers use the **Mender Crown**, while Mages may use a **Crow pet** for its "camouflage" perk, which grants up to 500 bonus defense after casting abilities [9, 14, 15].

---

## Q87: Explain the dungeon secrets system. How many secrets are in each floor, how do they affect loot, and how do players find them efficiently?

**Answer:**

The dungeon secrets system in Hypixel SkyBlock involves hidden items, chests, and interactions tucked away within the various rooms of the Catacombs. These secrets are fundamental to achieving high performance scores and unlocking the best possible rewards at the end of a run [1, 2].

### **The Role of Secrets in Dungeons**
Secrets are primarily used to increase a team's **Dungeon Score**. This score determines which loot chests appear after defeating a boss:
*   **Chest Requirements:** Higher-tier chests require higher scores. For instance, players must achieve a score of at least **230** to unlock the **Emerald Dungeon Chest** on Floor 1 [3].
*   **Loot Quality:** Better chests contain rarer rewards, such as **Recombobulator 3000s** or **Fuming Potato Books** on Floor 7 [4]. Additionally, high-tier loot like the **Spirit Mask** (now moved to Master Mode Floor 4) is gated behind dungeon score requirements [5].

### **Secret Counts and Scoring Changes**
The sources do not provide a specific list of the exact number of secrets found on every floor, as the number can vary based on the random generation of the dungeon layout. However, the system has undergone significant balancing:
*   **Reduced Requirements:** In late 2021, a patch **reduced the number of secrets necessary** on several floors to achieve a "full score" from secrets, making it easier for teams to reach maximum rewards without finding every single hidden item in a run [6].

### **Finding Secrets Efficiently**
Players use several built-in tools and strategies to locate secrets quickly:
*   **Map Indicators:** The in-game dungeon map provides visual cues for secret hunting. A **white check mark** appears on the map if a room still has undiscovered secrets, while a **green check mark** indicates that all secrets in that room have been found [1].
*   **Specialized Items:** New utility items have been introduced to assist with the process, such as the **Secret Tracker 3000** [7]. Other items like **Infiniboom TNT** are often used to blow up walls and reveal hidden passages efficiently [7].
*   **The clearing Meta:** Highly efficient teams often utilize "Hybrid" class setups—such as a **Hybrid Tank** using high-damage gear like Necron armor and Maxor boots—to clear rooms and find secrets at high speeds before switching to defensive "EHP" gear for the boss phase [8, 9].

### **Future Overhauls**
As of early 2026, there are indications that the dungeon system will receive a **modern overhaul** to fully utilize Minecraft version 1.21 features [10, 11]. This may include remade and remodeled dungeon creatures, such as the **Mimic**, and potentially a more fluid, native experience that departs from the legacy 1.8.9 room-based generator [11, 12].

## Q88: What is dungeon essence and how is it spent? Which essence types are most valuable and what should players spend it on first?

**Answer:**

In Hypixel SkyBlock, **Essence** is a specialized currency system used to upgrade items and purchase permanent profile or combat perks [1, 2]. While often associated with Dungeons, it has expanded to include several types tied to different areas of the game [3, 4].

### **What is Essence and How is it Obtained?**
Essence is a resource earned by participating in specific activities or by **salvaging** items related to that essence type [4, 5]. Major types found in the sources include:
*   **Gold and Diamond Essence:** Often rewarded through **Boss Collection** milestones [6].
*   **Dragon Essence:** Obtained by sacrificing or salvaging End and Dragon-related items at the NPC **Tyzzo** in the Dragon’s Nest [4].
*   **Ice Essence:** Earned by salvaging items like the **Frosted Scute** (100 essence) or the **Icy Sinker** (20 essence) [5, 7].
*   **Crimson Essence:** Primarily linked to the Crimson Isle and Kuudra content [3, 8].

### **How is Essence Spent?**
Essence is spent at dedicated **Essence Shops** or through specific NPCs to improve your character's capabilities [1, 9]:
*   **Item Upgrades:** It is used to "star" items, increasing their stats. For example, **Diamond Heads** can be purchased from Ophelia once unlocked, and they carry over upgrades from Golden Heads [2, 6].
*   **Permanent Perks:** You can purchase buffs that provide global benefits. In the Dragon Essence shop, players can max out the **Zeal perk**, which provides a global buff to spawning special zealots while in the End [10].
*   **Combat Specializations:** NPCs like **Vulcan** allow players to spend Crimson Essence on Kuudra-specific perks, such as **Head Start** (starting with extra tokens) and **Fresh Tools** (increasing the speed of repairing the ballista) [8].

### **Valuable Essence Types and Spending Priorities**
Based on the sources, the "most valuable" essence often depends on your stage of gameplay and current goals:

1.  **Crimson Essence (High Value for Profit):** Late-game players often prioritize **Crimson Essence** because it is a key component in "RNG-heavy" money-making methods like Kuudra, which are cited as superior long-term profit centers [11, 12].
2.  **Dragon Essence (Priority for Early/Mid-Game Money):** If you are farming Summoning Eyes, spending Dragon Essence on the **Zeal perk** is considered "basically free money" because it significantly stacks with other luck buffs to increase your hourly income [10].
3.  **Gold/Diamond Essence (Priority for Dungeons):** For those focusing on Dungeon progression, these are essential for acquiring and upgrading **Golden and Diamond Boss Heads**, which are meta equipment for their respective floors [2, 6].

**What to spend on first?**
*   **For Beginners:** Prioritize the **Zeal perk** in the Dragon Essence shop to boost Summoning Eye rates, as this is a simple and reliable early money-making method [10].
*   **For Advanced Combat Players:** Focus on the **Head Start 5** and **Fresh Tools** perks from the Crimson Essence shop to optimize Kuudra runs, which allows for more efficient clearing and building during the boss fight [8].

## Q89: What are the most valuable drops from dungeons? List the top 10 most valuable dungeon items and which floors they come from.

**Answer:**

While the value of items fluctuates in the SkyBlock economy, the most valuable dungeon drops are primarily concentrated in **Master Mode Floor 7 (M7)**, followed by **Floor 7 (F7)** and **Master Mode Floor 4 (M4)** [1, 2]. Current economic analysis suggests that M7 remains the most profitable dungeon tier, yielding approximately **50 million coins per hour** [1].

The following are the top 10 most valuable items dropped from dungeon reward chests as of early 2026:

1.  **Necron's Handle** (Floor 7 / Master Mode 7): This remains the "apex" drop used to craft the various Wither Blade variants, including the **Hyperion**, which is the top-tier weapon for Right-Click Mage (RCM) players [3-5].
2.  **Wither Scrolls** (Implosion, Shadow Warp, Wither Shield) (Floor 7 / Master Mode 7): These scrolls are required to apply "Wither Impact" to a Wither Blade. They are consistently featured in high-end price watches due to their necessity for endgame combat setups [4, 5].
3.  **Dark Claymore** (Master Mode Floor 7): Dropped exclusively from the final floor of the Catacombs, this is a top-tier melee weapon often cited in combat meta discussions and price-watch lists [5].
4.  **Spirit Mask** (Master Mode Floor 4): In a recent update, the Spirit Mask was removed from the Dark Auction and added to the **M4 Obsidian Chest**. It costs **15 million coins** to open the chest, and the item has significantly less weight than a Spirit Sword, maintaining its high value [2, 6].
5.  **Recombobulator 3000** (Floor 7 / Master Mode): A recurring high-value item from the Floor 7 loot pool that allows players to increase the rarity of their gear [5, 7].
6.  **Giant's Sword** (Floor 6 / Master Mode 6): Historically one of the most significant drops from the Floor 6 boss, Sadan, and remains a valuable melee option [8].
7.  **50/50 Skeleton Master Chestplate** (Master Mode Floor 7): A specific high-quality version of this armor is currently meta for endgame archers because its perk provides **infinite arrows**, removing a major friction point in high-speed runs [9].
8.  **Shadow Assassin Chestplate** (Floor 5 / Master Mode 5): This remains a highly desirable drop from the Floor 5 boss, Livid, serving as a staple for mid-to-late game combat progression [10].
9.  **Wither Armor Pieces** (Storm, Necron, Maxor, Goldor) (Floor 7 / Master Mode 7): While individual pieces vary in price, the full sets (especially Storm for Mages and Necron for melee/archers) are essential for Master Mode clearing and boss phases [3, 4, 9].
10. **Fuming Potato Book** (Floor 7 / Master Mode 7): A common but valuable addition to the Floor 7 loot chests that allows for further stat boosting on weapons and armor [7].

**Profitability Note:** While Floor 6 (M6) drops the Giant's Sword, community sentiment in 2026 views M6 as "worthless to run" compared to the higher hourly yields of M7 and the consistent profit from M4's Spirit Mask drops [1, 11].

## Q90: How does party composition affect dungeon income? What is the optimal 5-man composition for fast F7/M7 runs?

**Answer:**

Party composition primarily affects dungeon income by determining the **speed and efficiency of runs**; faster runs allow for more loot rolls per hour, which is the deterministic factor for RNG drops outside of static drop rates [1-3]. In 2026, the **Master Mode 7 (M7)** profit meta is estimated at **50M coins per hour**, while lower floors like M5 and M6 yield approximately 30M per hour [1].

### Optimal 5-Man Composition for F7/M7
The 2026 meta has shifted toward **"Hybrid" class roles** and the dominance of **Wither Blade variants** to maximize both clear speed and survivability [4].

*   **Archer:** The primary damage dealer uses a **Terminator** (with Duplex or Soul Eater) [5]. For high-speed runs, the meta involves the **Skeleton Master Chestplate** (specifically the 50/50 M7 version), which provides "infinite arrows" to remove friction during the run [5].
*   **Mage:** Utilizes a **Hyperion** (Ultimate Wise V) for right-click mage (RCM) clearing [4, 5]. Clearing gear typically consists of **Storm or Maxor armor** with **Wither Goggles** for maximum ability damage [5].
*   **Berserker:** Uses a **Valkyrie** or **Bouquet of Lies** [5]. This class has become the default dungeon class in recent updates [6].
*   **Hybrid Tank:** This role is critical for speed; the player uses a **high-damage clearing set** (3/4 Necron with Maxor boots and a Terminator) during the exploration phase [4]. They only switch to **"EHP" gear** (Goldor armor and a Reaper Mask) during high-pressure boss phases [4, 5]. Notably, the **Blue Whale** or **Turtle pet** has replaced the Baby Yeti for survivability, as the Baby Yeti's strength-to-defense perk was removed [7].
*   **Healer:** Typically wears **Goldor or Mender Crown** for boss phases and utilizes a **Wither Blade** or **Shortbow** for support [5].

### Economic Impact of Composition Efficiency
*   **Active Profitability:** Late-game players focus on "RNG-heavy" methods that leverage passive **Magic Find** buffs [8]. A highly efficient team can clear rooms faster, increasing the "kill-per-hour" rate needed to collect rare ingredients like **Funny Bones** (from Maxor/Storm phases) and **Brittle Powder** [9-11].
*   **Elimination of Swapping:** Since the 0.25.x update, Magic Find is a passive stat, meaning teams no longer lose time swapping to Magic Find gear at the end of a boss fight, further increasing the number of runs possible per hour [12-14].
*   **Friction Reduction:** Using specific gear like the Skeleton Master Chestplate allows the Archer to maintain maximum DPS without inventory management for arrows, contributing to the overall "Player Efficiency" that defines top-tier dungeon income [3, 5].

## Q91: What is the correct slayer unlock order? What are the prerequisites for unlocking Spider, Wolf, Enderman, Blaze, and Vampire slayer?

**Answer:**

The correct slayer unlock order generally follows the progression of combat levels and island access, starting with basic undead mobs and moving toward specialized late-game dimensions [1-3].

### **Correct Slayer Unlock Order**
While you can technically start several slayers at any time once you meet Maddox the Slayer, the community and game design suggest the following progression order based on difficulty and requirements:
1.  **Zombie Slayer** (Revenant Horror) [1]
2.  **Spider Slayer** (Tarantula Broodfather) [1]
3.  **Wolf Slayer** (Sven Packmaster) [1]
4.  **Enderman Slayer** (Voidgloom Seraph) [2]
5.  **Blaze Slayer** (Inferno Demonlord) [2]
6.  **Vampire Slayer** (Riftstalker Bloodfiend) [2]

---

### **Prerequisites for Unlocking Specific Slayers**

#### **1. Spider Slayer (Tarantula Broodfather)**
*   **Location:** Primarily fought in the **Spider's Den** [1, 4].
*   **Requirement:** There is no hard combat level requirement listed specifically for Spider Slayer, but players must travel through the Graveyard to reach the Spider's Den entrance [5, 6]. It is considered an early-to-mid-game combat pursuit [6].

#### **2. Wolf Slayer (Sven Packmaster)**
*   **Location:** Fought in **The Park** (specifically the castle ruins/cave areas) or the **Rift Dimension** [1, 7].
*   **Requirement:** Access to **The Park** is required, which is done through the Foraging section of the Hub [8].

#### **3. Enderman Slayer (Voidgloom Seraph)**
*   **Location:** Fought in **The End** [2, 9].
*   **Requirement:** Players must reach **Combat Level 12** to enter The End island [9, 10]. Beginners often use the **Void Sword** and a full **8-piece Ender Set** to survive these encounters [11, 12].

#### **4. Blaze Slayer (Inferno Demonlord)**
*   **Location:** Fought on the **Crimson Isle** [2, 13].
*   **Requirement:** Access to the **Crimson Isle** is necessary [13]. Reaching this island is difficult for early-game players as it has a high combat level requirement (typically **Combat 24**) and requires completing a **faction quest** (Mage or Barbarian) [14, 15].

#### **5. Vampire Slayer (Riftstalker Bloodfiend)**
*   **Location:** Fought exclusively within the **Rift Dimension** [2, 16].
*   **Requirement:** Players must **unlock the Rift Dimension**, which is considered a late-game goal [17]. Progressing here involves specific Rift-only gear and mechanics [16, 18].

### **General Slayer Tips**
*   **Quest Giver:** All slayer quests are initiated by talking to **Maddox** under the Tavern in the Hub [19, 20].
*   **Permanent Buffs:** Completing slayer tiers is essential for late-game progression because it provides **permanent stat boosts**, such as those from the **Year of the Witch** stews, which grant Magic Find against specific mob types [21, 22].
*   **Combat XP:** Farming slayers is a primary method for reaching high Combat levels, such as **Combat 60** [23].
